NEW LOCAL UI CONTRACT — implement in src/data/selection.ts:

```ts
import type { Analog, Comparison, Location, LocationsDataset } from '@shared/types/analog-location';
export interface ComparisonSelection {
  targetId: string;
  profileId: string;
  locationId: string;
}
export interface ResolvedComparisonSelection {
  selection: ComparisonSelection;
  comparison: Comparison;
  location: Location;
  analog: Analog | null;
}
export interface ComparisonSelectionOption extends ResolvedComparisonSelection {
  key: string;
  label: string;
}
export function selectionKey(selection: ComparisonSelection): string;
export function resolveSelection(
  dataset: LocationsDataset,
  selection: ComparisonSelection | null,
): ResolvedComparisonSelection | null;
export function listSelectionOptions(dataset: LocationsDataset): ComparisonSelectionOption[];
```

This is local presentation state, not a new dataset schema. Use an unambiguous key, e.g. JSON.stringify([targetId, profileId, locationId]); do not concatenate IDs with a delimiter that IDs can contain. The resolver selects the exact comparison then its target/analog; it does not search the first globally matching Earth ID. An ambiguous/absent exact comparison returns null, never silently selects a different target. Preserve deterministic ordering and valid selected context on reload; if selection disappears, choose a documented valid target/default or null.

[IMPLEMENTATION_SPEC]

1. CR001 — asynchronous renderer readiness must trigger dependent synchronization.

B03 initAfterPendingDispose awaits disposalChainRef before assigning renderer/scene/camera/group refs. The marker/body effect and mode/ground effect can run earlier, see null refs and return permanently. rendererEpoch only controls remount; assigning refs does not cause an effect rerun.

Add an explicit stateful readiness generation/epoch, published only after all core refs, controls, cleanup ownership and scheduling functions are installed for the current mount. Dependent effects subscribe to that readiness state. Distinguish ready-generation from requested rendererEpoch; do not create a render→remount loop. Every awaited init rechecks its captured generation/cancel flag. An old init must never publish ready or clear a newer owner's refs. Update globe material/markers and selected body after readiness even when dataset, selected and activeBody were already populated before mount. Ground initialization also resumes after readiness without requiring a user to reselect. Keep one renderer/canvas owner; disclosures do not recreate it.

2. CR002 — maintain comparison identity through every selection route.

Replace App's globally deduplicated selectedId/locationOptions lookup with ComparisonSelection and the pure resolver above. A shared Earth location may appear under multiple different target comparisons with different scores, ranks, profiles and excluded reasons. The dropdown must retain separate comparison-scoped options with labels that identify their parent target/profile; choosing the Earth analog for targetB must show targetB's score, breakdown, profile and source evidence.

Update HUD analog buttons, target controls and Viewport callbacks together. All selection callbacks carry enough context to resolve the exact comparison; no `find(first matching location.id)` fallback. An Earth globe marker can prefer the CURRENT comparison if that comparison contains the Earth site. If a physical marker has multiple contexts and no current matching context, make the ambiguity explicit/use the existing scoped selector; do not silently choose the first comparison. Do not fabricate markers/coordinates. Pegman still selects only sourced prepared AOIs. Selected context must not be lost when switching body, orbit/ground or opening Passport.

Maintain the current unique-target acceptance policy (CR003 deferred). Tests for this fix use two distinct target IDs sharing one consistent Earth location; do not introduce two comparisons with the same target ID to get the test accepted.

3. CR004 — correct coverage explanation without changing data or score.

In AnalogPassport/TelemetryHUD label coverage as `valid active-profile weight / total active-profile weight`. Ranked rows require coverage1 (display100%). It does not measure feature count or physical equivalence to the Moon/Mars. Separately display active numerical feature count `N/9`; use actual profile.id/version because ScoringProfile has no name field. Keep squared_distance_contribution labeled as normalized Euclidean squared-distance contribution, never percent contribution to the overall hybrid score. No scientific formula, dataset score, calibration bound or missing value may be rewritten by the UI.

4. CR008 — resource ownership, context restoration and stale telemetry.

- Hold the RoomEnvironment instance used by PMREM, dispose its geometry/material resources after environment map generation when no longer needed, dispose the PMREM generator and its generated render target/texture at the correct lifetime. Inspect existing Three0.170.0 API; do not invent disposal calls. Ensure each resource has one owner and is disposed once even through StrictMode and failed init.
- On contextlost, stop ground/orbit scheduling and input; on restored, either restart the single current owner's scheduling safely or recreate via the existing rendererEpoch path after serialized disposal. A restored ground view must resume its frame/input loop. A single direct draw is not a restored loop. Avoid duplicate RAF/timers and stale closures.
- Preserve abort tokens for pending heightfield/worker/splat loads. Serialize overlapping viewer loads/disposal and renderer cleanup; do not dispose the renderer while a prior viewer's async cleanup can still access it. Old generation finally blocks cannot clear a newer scene/owner.
- On ground→orbit and scene replacement, clear groundHudReadout position/elevation/slope/FPS/frame time and the ground-only triangle/datum/origin refs/status consistently, then emit cleared telemetry. Keep general DPR/device diagnostics only if actually measured. No stale terrain measurement may be presented as orbit data. Unlock pointer lock, clear held keys, abort load, dispose ground resources; no false hardware/60FPS certification.
- No perpetual orphan loop, geometry/material leak, duplicate listener or swallowed non-abort error. Preserve existing error UI and DEM fallback if splat overlay fails. Do not broaden splat support; payload trust fixes remain B05.

5. CR009 — implement metadata semantics in the browser validator, narrowly.

Read the immutable semantics below. JSON Schema alone is insufficient. Add pure checks in loadDataset.ts (or a small local semantic helper) and focused unit tests:

- Min/max inequality applies only to comparable extrema. Use recognized thermal FAMILY (`air_2m_temperature` versus `surface_temperature`), compatible temporal aggregation/method, matching observation period and spatial support/resolution. Do not require min.quantity===max.quantity: valid semantic identifiers can distinguish daily_min and daily_max. Conversely, do not compare surface minimum with air maximum or observations from different periods merely because both use degC.
- Define and document conservative family/aggregation classification using known identifiers from the actual contract/source context, including `air_2m_temperature_daily_min` / `air_2m_temperature_daily_max`; an unknown quantity is not silently assigned a family. For paired min/max method identifiers, accept only explicit compatible extrema variants or an identical aggregation method; do not strip arbitrary text until different methods become equal. Unknown/incomparable metadata cannot authorize a scientific ordering assertion. Such fields may remain separate observations if not used in a ranked comparison.
- For every ranked analog, all active profile features must have actual non-null finite target/analog values, the profile's quantity/unit, values inside independent bounds, and mutually compatible per-feature methods, periods, effective resolution and support. Comparing target.temp_min with analog.temp_min does require the same semantic quantity; the special thermal-family rule above concerns min-vs-max extrema within a location. Reject a ranked row with missing/incompatible active data; never fill, clip or recompute replacement observations.
- Active breakdown entries must be non-null with no missing reason; inactive entries must remain null with a reason. Enforce coverage1 for ranked rows under the all-active-features-required policy, using a documented tiny floating-point tolerance. A fractional coverage row cannot remain ranked because its score is present. Validate the per-feature similarity/contribution against the canonical normalized inputs/weights if implementing numeric consistency checks; validation must reject, not mutate values. Keep any such helper small and covered by independent expected-value tests. Do not add a new scoring policy or fit calibration from candidates.
- Validate references/unique profile keys and retain all existing body/frame/asset matrix checks. Do not remove the current unique-target rule under CR003. Do not move into streaming downloads, binary parsing or broad CLI verifier semantics; those are B05.

If platform records are still incomplete, report concrete failure and retain the issue for Astra's Windows check.

7. CR006 — resolve canonical shared contract consistently everywhere.

Vite/Vitest currently prefer sibling ../shared when present while TS paths list bundled ./shared first; verifier has its own resolution. Establish one explicit shared-root policy used by Vite, Vitest, TypeScript generated/resolved paths and verify-assets. In canonical monorepo mode sibling ../shared is authoritative; standalone sandbox may use ./shared only when no sibling canonical root exists. If both copies exist, compare schema and type bytes/hashes and FAIL on any mismatch; never silently compile against one and validate against the other. The canonical bytes below must stay unchanged.

TypeScript's fallback path order alone is not proof of shared-root agreement. Use a small deterministic preflight/generated relative-path config if necessary, with no new dependencies, and assert all tools report the same resolved root. Include files and exact exports for the helper in your implementation report. Generated config must be portable, not hardcoded to your sandbox absolute path.

Configure Vite fs access as the project root plus the explicitly chosen shared root; do not allow the repository's entire parent or filesystem root. Avoid scanning arbitrary sibling directories. Test monorepo and standalone layouts plus a mismatching duplicate copy in a temporary TEST_ONLY fixture. A missing schema/types must fail clearly before build. This is path/contract agreement only; full verify-assets binary/semantic hardening remains B05. No shared-file rewriting to make a mismatch disappear.

```typescript
/** Contract 1.0.0. Validate numeric bounds and cross-field invariants at runtime.
 * Heightmap invalid samples contain float 0 and mask 0; 0 is not a missing sentinel.
 */
export type Body = "earth" | "moon" | "mars";
export type FeatureKey =
  | "slope_mean_deg" | "slope_max_deg" | "roughness_index"
  | "elevation_delta_m" | "temp_min_c" | "temp_max_c"
  | "solar_irradiance_kwh_m2" | "aridity_index" | "albedo";
export type FeatureUnit = "deg" | "1" | "m" | "degC" | "kWh/m2/day";
export type Vec3 = [number, number, number];
export type Mat4 = [number, number, number, number, number, number, number, number,
  number, number, number, number, number, number, number, number];
export type LocalFrame = "local_enu_x_east_y_up_z_south";
export interface Source {
  id: string; provider: string; product_id: string; product_url: string;
  mission: string | null; access_url: string; retrieved_at: string;
  sha256: string; license: string; local_path: string;
}
export interface ObservationPeriod { start: string; end: string }
interface FeatureMetadata<U extends FeatureUnit> {
  unit: U; quantity: string; method: string;
  spatial_resolution_m: number | null; support_window_m: number | null;
  period: ObservationPeriod | null;
}
export type Feature<U extends FeatureUnit = FeatureUnit> = FeatureMetadata<U> & (
  | { value: number; source_ids: [string, ...string[]]; missing_reason: null }
  | { value: null; source_ids: string[]; missing_reason: string }
);
export interface Features {
  slope_mean_deg: Feature<"deg">; slope_max_deg: Feature<"deg">;
  roughness_index: Feature<"1">; elevation_delta_m: Feature<"m">;
  temp_min_c: Feature<"degC">; temp_max_c: Feature<"degC">;
  solar_irradiance_kwh_m2: Feature<"kWh/m2/day">;
  aridity_index: Feature<"1">; albedo: Feature<"1">;
}
export type CoordinateFrame = "earth_wgs84" | "moon_iau_planetocentric" | "mars_iau_planetocentric";
export interface Coordinates {
  latitude_deg: number; longitude_deg: number; frame: CoordinateFrame;
  source_ids: [string, ...string[]];
}
export interface AreaOfInterest { local_path: string; source_ids: [string, ...string[]] }
export interface Asset {
  path: string; sha256: string; bytes: number; source_ids: [string, ...string[]];
}
export interface Heightmap {
  asset: Asset; valid_mask: Asset; width: number; height: number;
  encoding: "float32_le"; nodata: null;
  row_order: "north_to_south"; col_order: "west_to_east"; samples: "grid_nodes";
  height_reference: "offset_from_origin_elevation_m";
  extent_m: [number, number, number, number];
  origin: { latitude_deg: number; longitude_deg: number; elevation_m: number };
  frame: LocalFrame; vertical_datum: string; meters_per_unit: 1;
}
export interface Splat {
  asset: Asset; format: "splat" | "ply"; gaussian_count: number;
  scale_is_metric: boolean; world_from_asset: Mat4; reconstruction_method: string;
  geometry_provenance: "photogrammetric" | "inferred";
}
export interface Camera {
  position: Vec3; lookAt: Vec3; frame: LocalFrame; fov: number; near: number; far: number;
}
export type Geology =
  | { classes: [string, ...string[]]; method: string; source_ids: [string, ...string[]]; missing_reason: null }
  | { classes: []; method: string; source_ids: string[]; missing_reason: string };
export interface Location<B extends Body = Body> {
  id: string; name: string; body: B; coordinates: Coordinates;
  aoi: AreaOfInterest; missions: string[];
  features: Features; geology: Geology;
  assets: { dem: Asset | null; heightmap: Heightmap | null; splat: Splat | null };
  camera: Camera | null;
}
export type FeatureBreakdown =
  | { similarity_pct: number; squared_distance_contribution: number; reason: null }
  | { similarity_pct: null; squared_distance_contribution: null; reason: string };
export interface Analog {
  location: Location<"earth">; similarity_score: number; rank: 1 | 2 | 3;
  breakdown: Record<FeatureKey, FeatureBreakdown>; coverage: number;
}
export interface Excluded { location_id: string; reasons: [string, ...string[]] }
type ProfileFeatureBase<K extends FeatureKey, U extends FeatureUnit> = {
  key: K; unit: U; quantity: string; weight: number; lower: number; upper: number;
  calibration_source_ids: [string, ...string[]]; calibration_method: string;
};
export type ProfileFeature =
  | ProfileFeatureBase<"slope_mean_deg" | "slope_max_deg", "deg">
  | ProfileFeatureBase<"elevation_delta_m", "m">
  | ProfileFeatureBase<"temp_min_c" | "temp_max_c", "degC">
  | ProfileFeatureBase<"solar_irradiance_kwh_m2", "kWh/m2/day">
  | ProfileFeatureBase<"roughness_index" | "aridity_index" | "albedo", "1">;
export interface ScoringProfile {
  id: string; version: string; algorithm: "weighted_cosine_euclidean_v1";
  alpha: number; features: [ProfileFeature, ...ProfileFeature[]];
  comparison_resolution_m: number; window_m: number; min_valid_area_fraction: number;
}
export interface Comparison {
  target: Location<"moon" | "mars">; profile_id: string;
  analogs: Analog[]; excluded: Excluded[];
}
export interface LocationsDataset {
  schema_version: "1.0.0"; generated_at: string;
  sources: Source[]; profiles: ScoringProfile[]; comparisons: Comparison[];
}
```

===== IMMUTABLE CANONICAL SEMANTICS (full file) =====
```markdown
# Contract 1.0.0 — runtime invariants

JSON Schema governs structure, types, bounds and required missing-data reasons. It does not prove scientific validity. Python and the browser must also validate the relationships below. TypeScript interfaces are compile-time shapes only; JSON received at runtime requires validation.

- Unique source/profile/location IDs and complete referential integrity. Every non-null measurement, location geometry and derived asset has actual input provenance. A catalog reference is not a source observation.
- `analog.similarity_score` exists only for ranked Earth locations. Unrankable candidates appear in `excluded` with reasons; no numeric placeholder is emitted. Targets with invalid profiles have no ranked analogs. `analogs` is sorted by full-precision descending score then location ID, with consecutive ranks 1..N and N<=3.
- Every ranked row uses the same immutable scoring profile, quantities, units, effective resolution, physical support window, methods and temporal aggregation. All profile features are required. Profile bounds are versioned independent calibration, never refitted to current candidates. Check lower<upper, unique keys, finite inputs, bounds and source references. No silent clipping. Profile changes need a version change.
- `coverage` means valid active-profile weight / total active-profile weight. Ranked entries therefore have coverage 1. It is not full physical coverage of a Moon/Mars environment. Display active features out of the nine numerical features and the profile name. Geology is a separate categorical field, excluded from LOOP_0 arithmetic.
- `Feature.quantity` is a semantic identifier, including surface versus 2 m air temperature and albedo spectral/illumination definition. `method` records derivation and aggregation; period uses UTC timestamps, start<=end. Surface-temperature extrema from different coverage/aggregation are not automatically comparable. Missing is null with reason; zero remains a real value.
- Numeric features have canonical units in schema. `solar_irradiance_kwh_m2` is a legacy API field name for mean daily irradiation in kWh/m2/day, not instantaneous irradiance. Do not change its meaning silently.
- Mean slope<=max slope; min temperature<=max temperature where comparable. Finite values only. Coordinate source frame must be converted, not relabelled. Longitude is east-positive in [-180,180]. Heightmap origin uses the location body's coordinate frame. Planetary CRS parameters and input datum/labels remain in source sidecars.
- `aoi.local_path` is a same-origin `/assets/...` URL to the published real AOI GeoJSON. Its source records identify original geometry. GeoJSON lon/lat is interpreted in the explicit location body/frame; do not silently treat planetary geometry as WGS84. Validate rings, coordinate ranges, closure, antimeridian handling and membership offline. Raw source `local_path` is relative to repository root.
- Source sidecars preserve CRS/datum, vertical units, no-data value, product version, observation time, quality flags, original labels and transformation method. `Source.local_path` identifies actual cached raw bytes and must match its SHA256. Document companion labels and sidecars in the source manifest with their own checksums.
- Heightmaps are grid nodes in row-major order. x increases west→east, z increases north→south, y is up, all metres. `extent_m=[x_min,z_min,x_max,z_max]`, minima<maxima; spacing=(max-min)/(count-1). Height floats are offsets from `origin.elevation_m`; values are finite little-endian Float32. Mask is Uint8 (0 invalid,1 valid), exactly width*height bytes. Height buffer is exactly width*height*4 bytes. A zero at mask=0 is storage padding, not an observation. Do not interpolate or allow collision across invalid nodes.
- Camera position/lookAt are local metres with y relative to origin. Require near<far and nonzero viewing direction; validate initial position over valid supported terrain before ground entry. FOV and eye height are UI settings, not NASA observations.
- `Splat.world_from_asset` is a Three.js column-major 4x4 affine matrix into the heightmap frame. Validate finite, nonsingular transform; reject unsupported shear/reflection rather than silently misregistering it. Its translation is metres. Splats used for metric movement require metric scale and real registration. `inferred` geometry cannot provide scientific measurements; LOOP_0 ground navigation requires a sourced DEM and measured registration. A plain point-cloud PLY is not automatically a Gaussian PLY.
- Resolve local paths and reject escapes from the intended repository/public roots, including symlinks. Verify hashes, file sizes and file formats before publication and before expensive browser allocation. Asset budgets are engineering policy, never altered scientific heights.
- A neutral orbital sphere is a UI reference surface, not a DEM. A Pegman drop only selects an existing prepared AOI, with snapping visibly disclosed. An unsupported drop yields no measurement or score. Sun azimuth and horizon are N/A until their inputs and algorithm exist.

## Scoring definition

For common normalized vectors x,y in [0,1], positive weights w and W=sum(w):

`D=sqrt(sum(w*(x-y)^2)/W)`; `C=sum(w*x*y)/sqrt(sum(w*x*x)*sum(w*y*y))`.

Both zero norms give C=1; exactly one zero norm gives C=0. `score=100*(alpha*C+(1-alpha)*(1-D))`. This is a project-defined similarity index, not a NASA-approved habitability probability. Round only for display.

Per-feature percentage is `100*(1-abs(x-y))`; squared-distance contribution is `w*(x-y)^2/W`. Percentages are not additive contributions to the total score.

## State gate

LOOP_0 preparation does not complete either implementation module. Only explicit human `[PASS: Dev_A/...]` and `[PASS: Dev_B/...]` permit PHASE 5. Any FAIL/PERF_WARN pauses progression and produces a targeted hotfix prompt. Do not treat an empty dataset or skipped WebGL test as demo acceptance.
```

      "content": "# Planetary Analog Explorer — Web Client (`LOOP_0 / DEV_B`)\n\n3D viewport, DEM heightfield terrain engine, Gaussian Splat overlay pipeline, and scientific telemetry HUD for Planetary Analog Explorer.\n\n## Attribution & Review Status\n\nThe modules listed below were generated by **Gemini Hydrogen** for `LOOP_0 / DEV_B` (through `B03`) and are **pending human review**:\n\n- `src/components/Viewport3D.tsx`\n- `src/components/TelemetryHUD.tsx`\n- `src/components/AnalogPassport.tsx`\n- `src/components/ui/InfoDisclosure.tsx`\n- `src/App.tsx`\n- `src/main.tsx`\n- `src/styles.css`\n- `src/data/loadDataset.ts`\n- `src/terrain/coordinates.ts`\n- `src/terrain/heightfield.ts`\n- `src/terrain/splatUtils.ts`\n- `src/terrain/terrain.worker.ts`\n- `src/terrain/terrain-worker-client.ts`\n- `src/types/gaussian-splats-3d.d.ts`\n- `src/data/loadDataset.test.ts`\n- `src/terrain/heightfield.test.ts`\n- `scripts/verify-assets.mjs`\n- `tests/viewport.spec.ts`\n- `tests/ui-sdk.spec.ts`\n- `tests/agent-harness.html`\n- `tests/agent-harness.tsx`\n\nAfter copying and reviewing these files in `planetary-analog-explorer/web-client/`, the developer must record verified entries in `AI_AND_DATA_ATTRIBUTION_LOG`.\n\n## Pinned Dependencies\n\nAll mandated packages use exact versions (no `^` or `~`). In `B03`, the React baseline was intentionally updated with Astra approval to satisfy `@ui-sdk/react` peer requirements:\n\n- `react` `19.3.1` & `react-dom` `19.3.1` (`@types/react` `19.3.0`, `@types/react-dom` `19.3.0`).\n- `@ui-sdk/core` `0.1.0-beta.1` & `@ui-sdk/react` `0.1.0-beta.1` (Apache-2.0 licensed beta pre-release; used locally in `InfoDisclosure.tsx` and test-only `createAgent` harness — no remote scripts, WebMCP endpoints, or external AI calls).\n- `@types/node` `22.14.0` (updated from `22.10.2` to satisfy `vite@8.3.8` / `vitest@5.1.0` `peerOptional @types/node >=22.12.0` cleanly without `--force` or `--legacy-peer-deps`).\n- `lucide-react` `0.546.0` — UI affordance icons (`App.tsx`, `Viewport3D.tsx`, `TelemetryHUD.tsx`).\n- `@tailwindcss/vite` `4.3.5` & `tailwindcss` `4.3.5` — local CSS build plugin and Tailwind v4 utility compilation (`vite.config.ts`, `src/styles.css`).\n\nNo Gemini runtime SDK, Express server, dotenv, or external API key handling is included.\n\n## Coordinate Frames & Heightfield Layout\n\n- **Local ENU Frame (`local_enu_x_east_y_up_z_south`)**: `+X` East, `+Y` Up, `+Z` South, 1 unit = 1 metre.\n- **Row-Major Grid Ordering**: `row = 0..height-1` runs `north_to_south` (`z = extent_m[1] + row * spacingZ_m`); `col = 0..width-1` runs `west_to_east` (`x = extent_m[0] + col * spacingX_m`), where `spacing = (max - min) / (count - 1)`.\n- **Vertical Origin**: Binary `float32_le` values in `heights` are local offsets in metres relative to `origin.elevation_m`. `sampleHeight()` returns local `y` directly for Three.js camera/mesh collision; absolute datum elevation (`y + origin.elevation_m`) is computed only for HUD display.\n- **NoData Handling**: Nodes with `valid_mask === 0` are excluded from triangle generation and collision sampling (`sampleHeight()` returns `null`). Zero (`0`) is never treated as a missing sentinel.\n\n## Engineering Budgets\n\n- Max DPR: `1.5` (with adaptive hysteresis step-down on sustained frame times > 24 ms).\n- Max terrain mesh grid: `257 × 257` nodes (`66,049` nodes).\n- Max Gaussian splats: `250,000` splats.\n- Max active ground scene assets: `64 MiB`.\n\n## Empty State & Real Dev_A Dataset\n\nWith no `public/data/locations_dataset.json` present, the UI enters an honest empty/error state with a Retry button and renders only the neutral **Schematic Reference Globe** in Orbit mode. No fabricated coordinates, terrain, or similarity scores are generated.\n\n## Verification & Dev Server Commands\n\nBy default, `npm run dev` binds to loopback (`127.0.0.1:3000`). If an external sandbox proxy (such as AI Studio container hosting) requires binding all interfaces, use `npm run dev:sandbox` (`vite --port 3000 --host 0.0.0.0`) separately.\n\n```powershell\nnode --version\nnpm install\nnpm run typecheck\nnpm run test\nnpm run build\nnpx playwright install chromium\nnpm run e2e\nnpm run verify:assets\nnpm run dev\n```\n"
    },

      "content": "<!doctype html>\n<html lang=\"en\" class=\"dark\">\n  <head>\n    <meta charset=\"UTF-8\" />\n    <meta name=\"viewport\" content=\"width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0\" />\n    <title>Planetary Analog Explorer</title>\n    <meta\n      name=\"description\"\n      content=\"3D viewport and telemetry explorer for planetary analog sites across Earth, the Moon, and Mars.\"\n    />\n    <meta property=\"og:title\" content=\"Planetary Analog Explorer\" />\n    <meta\n      property=\"og:description\"\n      content=\"3D viewport and telemetry explorer for planetary analog sites across Earth, the Moon, and Mars.\"\n    />\n    <meta property=\"og:type\" content=\"website\" />\n    <meta name=\"twitter:card\" content=\"summary_large_image\" />\n  </head>\n  <body class=\"bg-[#07090E] text-slate-100 antialiased selection:bg-cyan-500/30\">\n    <div id=\"root\"></div>\n    <script type=\"module\" src=\"/src/main.tsx\"></script>\n  </body>\n</html>\n"

      "path": "package.json",
      "bytes": 1156,
      "sha256": "565961ff0a939e6acfb5c107a16d1ceea414cee534e94fc342a47cd9b8acad04",
      "content": "{\n  \"name\": \"planetary-analog-explorer-web\",\n  \"private\": true,\n  \"version\": \"0.1.0\",\n  \"type\": \"module\",\n  \"scripts\": {\n    \"dev\": \"vite --port 3000 --host 127.0.0.1\",\n    \"dev:sandbox\": \"vite --port 3000 --host 0.0.0.0\",\n    \"build\": \"vite build\",\n    \"preview\": \"vite preview --host 127.0.0.1\",\n    \"typecheck\": \"tsc --noEmit\",\n    \"test\": \"vitest run\",\n    \"e2e\": \"playwright test\",\n    \"verify:assets\": \"node scripts/verify-assets.mjs\",\n    \"clean\": \"rm -rf dist\",\n    \"lint\": \"tsc --noEmit\"\n  },\n  \"dependencies\": {\n    \"@mkkellogg/gaussian-splats-3d\": \"0.4.7\",\n    \"@ui-sdk/core\": \"0.1.0-beta.1\",\n    \"@ui-sdk/react\": \"0.1.0-beta.1\",\n    \"ajv\": \"8.17.1\",\n    \"ajv-formats\": \"3.0.1\",\n    \"lucide-react\": \"0.546.0\",\n    \"react\": \"19.3.1\",\n    \"react-dom\": \"19.3.1\",\n    \"three\": \"0.170.0\"\n  },\n  \"devDependencies\": {\n    \"@playwright/test\": \"1.65.1\",\n    \"@tailwindcss/vite\": \"4.3.5\",\n    \"@types/node\": \"22.14.0\",\n    \"@types/react\": \"19.3.0\",\n    \"@types/react-dom\": \"19.3.0\",\n    \"@types/three\": \"0.170.0\",\n    \"@vitejs/plugin-react\": \"6.3.0\",\n    \"tailwindcss\": \"4.3.5\",\n    \"typescript\": \"5.9.3\",\n    \"vite\": \"8.3.8\",\n    \"vitest\": \"5.1.0\"\n  }\n}\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "playwright.config.ts",
      "bytes": 917,
      "sha256": "4cbfeae175e4afc45d02be5300390bc932a6ef5248733d584aac8a4d386c3a77",
      "content": "import fs from 'node:fs';\nimport { defineConfig, devices } from '@playwright/test';\n\nconst containerHeadlessShell = '/ms-playwright/chrome-headless-shell-linux64/chrome-headless-shell';\nconst executablePath = fs.existsSync(containerHeadlessShell) ? containerHeadlessShell : undefined;\n\nexport default defineConfig({\n  testDir: './tests',\n  fullyParallel: false,\n  retries: 0,\n  reporter: 'list',\n  use: {\n    baseURL: 'http://127.0.0.1:4173',\n    headless: true,\n    trace: 'retain-on-failure',\n    launchOptions: executablePath ? { executablePath } : undefined,\n  },\n  projects: [\n    {\n      name: 'chromium',\n      use: {\n        ...devices['Desktop Chrome'],\n        viewport: { width: 1440, height: 900 },\n      },\n    },\n  ],\n  webServer: {\n    command: 'npm run preview -- --port 4173 --host 127.0.0.1',\n    url: 'http://127.0.0.1:4173',\n    reuseExistingServer: !process.env.CI,\n    timeout: 30_000,\n  },\n});\n"
    },

      "content": "{\n  \"task_id\": \"RESOURCE-01\",\n  \"role\": \"Dev_B\",\n  \"contract_version\": \"1.0.0\",\n  \"input_bundle_sha256\":       "path": "scripts/verify-assets.mjs",
      "bytes": 6757,
      "sha256": "6420359e53183e246b29817832bd360f79f4407fce56e98ddd248a4b4a1e8e8e",
      "content": "import crypto from 'node:crypto';\nimport fs from 'node:fs';\nimport path from 'node:path';\nimport { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';\nimport Ajv2020 from 'ajv/dist/2020.js';\nimport addFormats from 'ajv-formats';\n\nconst __filename = fileURLToPath(import.meta.url);\nconst __dirname = path.dirname(__filename);\nconst webClientRoot = path.resolve(__dirname, '..');\nconst publicRoot = path.join(webClientRoot, 'public');\n\nconst MAX_GRID_NODES = 257 * 257;\nconst MAX_SPLATS = 250_000;\nconst MAX_SCENE_BYTES = 64 * 1024 * 1024;\n\nfunction resolveSchemaPath() {\n  const monorepoPath = path.resolve(webClientRoot, '../shared/schemas/analog_location.schema.json');\n  if (fs.existsSync(monorepoPath)) return monorepoPath;\n  const localPath = path.resolve(webClientRoot, 'shared/schemas/analog_location.schema.json');\n  if (fs.existsSync(localPath)) return localPath;\n  throw new Error('Could not find shared/schemas/analog_location.schema.json');\n}\n\nfunction resolveSafePublicPath(assetUrlPath) {\n  if (typeof assetUrlPath !== 'string' || !assetUrlPath.startsWith('/assets/')) {\n    throw new Error(`Invalid asset URL path (must start with /assets/): ${assetUrlPath}`);\n  }\n  const rel = assetUrlPath.slice(1);\n  const resolved = path.resolve(publicRoot, rel);\n  const realPublic = fs.existsSync(publicRoot) ? fs.realpathSync(publicRoot) : publicRoot;\n  if (!resolved.startsWith(realPublic + path.sep)) {\n    throw new Error(`Path traversal detected for asset: ${assetUrlPath}`);\n  }\n  if (!fs.existsSync(resolved)) {\n    throw new Error(`Missing asset file on disk: ${resolved}`);\n  }\n  const realTarget = fs.realpathSync(resolved);\n  if (!realTarget.startsWith(realPublic + path.sep)) {\n    throw new Error(`Symlink escapes public/ directory: ${assetUrlPath}`);\n  }\n  return realTarget;\n}\n\nfunction verifyFileHashAndSize(filePath, expectedBytes, expectedSha256, label) {\n  const stat = fs.statSync(filePath);\n  if (!stat.isFile()) {\n    throw new Error(`${label} is not a regular file: ${filePath}`);\n  }\n  if (typeof expectedBytes === 'number' && stat.size !== expectedBytes) {\n    throw new Error(\n      `${label} byte count mismatch: expected ${expectedBytes}, found ${stat.size} (${filePath})`,\n    );\n  }\n  if (typeof expectedSha256 === 'string') {\n    const buf = fs.readFileSync(filePath);\n    const actualSha = crypto.createHash('sha256').update(buf).digest('hex');\n    if (actualSha !== expectedSha256.toLowerCase()) {\n      throw new Error(\n        `${label} SHA-256 mismatch: expected ${expectedSha256}, found ${actualSha} (${filePath})`,\n      );\n    }\n  }\n}\n\nfunction verifyLocationAssets(loc, label) {\n  let sceneBytes = 0;\n\n  if (!loc.aoi?.local_path) {\n    throw new Error(`${label}: missing aoi.local_path`);\n  }\n  const aoiPath = resolveSafePublicPath(loc.aoi.local_path);\n  if (!aoiPath.endsWith('.geojson') && !aoiPath.endsWith('.json')) {\n    throw new Error(`${label}: unexpected AOI format ${aoiPath}`);\n  }\n\n  if (loc.assets.dem) {\n    const demPath = resolveSafePublicPath(loc.assets.dem.path);\n    verifyFileHashAndSize(demPath, loc.assets.dem.bytes, loc.assets.dem.sha256, `${label}.dem`);\n  }\n\n  if (loc.assets.heightmap) {\n    const hm = loc.assets.heightmap;\n    if (hm.width * hm.height > MAX_GRID_NODES) {\n      throw new Error(\n        `${label}.heightmap: ${hm.width}x${hm.height} exceeds ${MAX_GRID_NODES} node budget`,\n      );\n    }\n    if (hm.asset.bytes !== hm.width * hm.height * 4) {\n      throw new Error(`${label}.heightmap.asset.bytes != width*height*4`);\n    }\n    if (hm.valid_mask.bytes !== hm.width * hm.height) {\n      throw new Error(`${label}.heightmap.valid_mask.bytes != width*height`);\n    }\n    const hPath = resolveSafePublicPath(hm.asset.path);\n    const mPath = resolveSafePublicPath(hm.valid_mask.path);\n    verifyFileHashAndSize(hPath, hm.asset.bytes, hm.asset.sha256, `${label}.heightmap.asset`);\n    verifyFileHashAndSize(\n      mPath,\n      hm.valid_mask.bytes,\n      hm.valid_mask.sha256,\n      `${label}.heightmap.valid_mask`,\n    );\n    sceneBytes += hm.asset.bytes + hm.valid_mask.bytes;\n  }\n\n  if (loc.assets.splat) {\n    const sp = loc.assets.splat;\n    if (sp.format !== 'splat' && sp.format !== 'ply') {\n      throw new Error(`${label}.splat: unsupported format \"${sp.format}\"`);\n    }\n    if (sp.gaussian_count > MAX_SPLATS) {\n      throw new Error(\n        `${label}.splat: gaussian_count (${sp.gaussian_count}) exceeds ${MAX_SPLATS}`,\n      );\n    }\n    const sPath = resolveSafePublicPath(sp.asset.path);\n    verifyFileHashAndSize(sPath, sp.asset.bytes, sp.asset.sha256, `${label}.splat.asset`);\n    sceneBytes += sp.asset.bytes;\n  }\n\n  if (sceneBytes > MAX_SCENE_BYTES) {\n    throw new Error(`${label}: combined active ground assets (${sceneBytes} B) exceed 64 MiB`);\n  }\n}\n\nfunction main() {\n  const datasetPath = path.join(publicRoot, 'data', 'locations_dataset.json');\n  if (!fs.existsSync(datasetPath)) {\n    console.error(\n      `[verify:assets] FAIL: Missing prepared dataset at ${datasetPath}. Dev_A must publish public/data/locations_dataset.json and public/assets/ before demo verification.`,\n    );\n    process.exit(1);\n  }\n\n  const schemaPath = resolveSchemaPath();\n  const schema = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(schemaPath, 'utf8'));\n  const dataset = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(datasetPath, 'utf8'));\n\n  const ajv = new Ajv2020({ allErrors: true, strict: false });\n  addFormats(ajv);\n  const validate = ajv.compile(schema);\n  if (!validate(dataset)) {\n    console.error('[verify:assets] FAIL: JSON Schema validation failed:');\n    for (const err of validate.errors ?? []) {\n      console.error(`  - ${err.instancePath || '$'} ${err.message}`);\n    }\n    process.exit(1);\n  }\n\n  if (!Array.isArray(dataset.comparisons) || dataset.comparisons.length === 0) {\n    console.error('[verify:assets] FAIL: Dataset contains zero comparisons.');\n    process.exit(1);\n  }\n\n  let verifiedTargets = 0;\n  let verifiedGroundTargets = 0;\n\n  for (let i = 0; i < dataset.comparisons.length; i += 1) {\n    const comp = dataset.comparisons[i];\n    verifyLocationAssets(comp.target, `comparisons[${i}].target(${comp.target.id})`);\n    verifiedTargets += 1;\n    if (comp.target.assets.heightmap && comp.target.camera) {\n      verifiedGroundTargets += 1;\n    }\n    for (let j = 0; j < comp.analogs.length; j += 1) {\n      const analog = comp.analogs[j];\n      verifyLocationAssets(\n        analog.location,\n        `comparisons[${i}].analogs[${j}](${analog.location.id})`,\n      );\n    }\n  }\n\n  console.log(\n    `[verify:assets] PASS: Verified ${verifiedTargets} target(s) (${verifiedGroundTargets} ground-ready) against schema and SHA-256 budgets.`,\n  );\n}\n\ntry {\n  main();\n} catch (err) {\n  console.error(`[verify:assets] FAIL: ${err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err)}`);\n  process.exit(1);\n}\n"
    },

   \"features\": {\"type\":\"object\",\"additionalProperties\":false,\"required\":[\"slope_mean_deg\",\"slope_max_deg\",\"roughness_index\",\"elevation_delta_m\",\"temp_min_c\",\"temp_max_c\",\"solar_irradiance_kwh_m2\",\"aridity_index\",\"albedo\"],\"properties\":{\"slope_mean_deg\":{\"allOf\":[{\"$ref\":\"#/$defs/feature\"},{\"properties\":{\"unit\":{\"const\":\"deg\"},\"value\":{\"type\":[\"number\",\"null\"],\"minimum\":0,\"maximum\":90}}}]},\"slope_max_deg\":{\"allOf\":[{\"$ref\":\"#/$defs/feature\"},{\"properties\":{\"unit\":{\"const\":\"deg\"},\"value\":{\"type\":[\"number\",\"null\"],\"minimum\":0,\"maximum\":90}}}]},\"roughness_index\":{\"allOf\":[{\"$ref\":\"#/$defs/feature\"},{\"properties\":{\"unit\":{\"const\":\"1\"},\"value\":{\"type\":[\"number\",\"null\"],\"minimum\":0}}}]},\"elevation_delta_m\":{\"allOf\":[{\"$ref\":\"#/$defs/feature\"},{\"properties\":{\"unit\":{\"const\":\"m\"},\"value\":{\"type\":[\"number\",\"null\"],\"minimum\":0}}}]},\"temp_min_c\":{\"allOf\":[{\"$ref\":\"#/$defs/feature\"},{\"properties\":{\"unit\":{\"const\":\"degC\"},\"value\":{\"type\":[\"number\",\"null\"],\"minimum\":-273.15}}}]},\"temp_max_c\":{\"allOf\":[{\"$ref\":\"#/$defs/feature\"},{\"properties\":{\"unit\":{\"const\":\"degC\"},\"value\":{\"type\":[\"number\",\"null\"],\"minimum\":-273.15}}}]},\"solar_irradiance_kwh_m2\":{\"allOf\":[{\"$ref\":\"#/$defs/feature\"},{\"properties\":{\"unit\":{\"const\":\"kWh/m2/day\"},\"value\":{\"type\":[\"number\",\"null\"],\"minimum\":0}}}]},\"aridity_index\":{\"allOf\":[{\"$ref\":\"#/$defs/feature\"},{\"properties\":{\"unit\":{\"const\":\"1\"},\"value\":{\"type\":[\"number\",\"null\"],\"minimum\":0}}}]},\"albedo\":{\"allOf\":[{\"$ref\":\"#/$defs/feature\"},{\"properties\":{\"unit\":{\"const\":\"1\"},\"value\":{\"type\":[\"number\",\"null\"],\"minimum\":0,\"maximum\":1}}}]}}},\n {\"type\":\"object\",\"additionalProperties\":false,\"required\":[\"classes\",\"method\",\"source_ids\",\"missing_reason\"],\"properties\":{\"classes\":{\"type\":\"array\",\"items\":{\"type\":\"string\",\"minLength\":1},\"uniqueItems\":true},\"method\":{\"type\":\"string\",\"minLength\":1},\"source_ids\":{\"type\":\"array\",\"items\":{\"type\":\"string\",\"minLength\":1},\"uniqueItems\":true},\"missing_reason\":{\"type\":[\"string\",\"null\"],\"minLength\":1}},\"allOf\":[{\"if\":{\"properties\":{\"classes\":{\"minItems\":1}}},\"then\":{\"properties\":{\"source_ids\":{\"type\":\"array\",\"items\":{\"type\":\"string\",\"minLength\":1},\"uniqueItems\":true,\"minItems\":1},\"missing_reason\":{\"type\":\"null\"}}},\"else\":{\"properties\":{\"missing_reason\":{\"type\":\"string\",\"minLength\":1}}}}]},\n    \"location\": {\"type\":\"object\",\"additionalProperties\":false,\"required\":[\"id\",\"name\",\"body\",\"coordinates\",\"aoi\",\"missions\",\"features\",\"geology\",\"assets\",\"camera\"],\"properties\":{\"id\":{\"type\":\"string\",\"minLength\":1},\"name\":{\"type\":\"string\",\"minLength\":1},\"body\":{\"enum\":[\"earth\",\"moon\",\"mars\"]},\"coordinates\":{\"$ref\":\"#/$defs/coordinates\"},\"aoi\":{\"type\":\"object\",\"additionalProperties\":false,\"required\":[\"local_path\",\"source_ids\"],\"properties\":{\"local_path\":{\"type\":\"string\",\"pattern\":\"^/assets/(?!\\\\.{1,2}(?:/|$))(?!.*\\\\/\\\\.{1,2}(?:/|$))[A-Za-z0-9_./-]+$\"},\"source_ids\":{\"type\":\"array\",\"items\":{\"type\":\"string\",\"minLength\":1},\"uniqueItems\":true,\"minItems\":1}}},\"missions\":{\"type\":\"array\",\"items\":{\"type\":\"string\",\"minLength\":1},\"uniqueItems\":true},\"features\":{\"$ref\":\"#/$defs/features\"},\"geology\":{\"$ref\":\"#/$defs/geology\"},\"assets\":{\"type\":\"object\",\"additionalProperties\":false,\"required\":[\"dem\",\"heightmap\",\"splat\"],\"properties\":{\"dem\":{\"anyOf\":[{\"$ref\":\"#/$defs/asset\"},{\"type\":\"null\"}]},\"heightmap\":{\"anyOf\":[{\"$ref\":\"#/$defs/heightmap\"},{\"type\":\"null\"}]},\"splat\":{\"anyOf\":[{\"$ref\":\"#/$defs/splat\"},{\"type\":\"null\"}]}}},\"camera\":{\"anyOf\":[{\"$ref\":\"#/$defs/camera\"},{\"type\":\"null\"}]}},\"allOf\":[{\"if\":{\"properties\":{\"body\":{\"const\":\"earth\"}}},\"then\":{\"properties\":{\"coordinates\":{\"properties\":{\"frame\":{\"const\":\"earth_wgs84\"}}}}}},{\"if\":{\"properties\":{\"body\":{\"const\":\"moon\"}}},\"then\":{\"properties\":{\"coordinates\":{\"properties\":{\"frame\":{\"const\":\"moon_iau_planetocentric\"}}}}}},{\"if\":{\"properties\":{\"body\":{\"const\":\"mars\"}}},\"then\":{\"properties\":{\"coordinates\":{\"properties\":{\"frame\":{\"const\":\"mars_iau_planetocentric\"}}}}}}]},\n    \"featureBreakdown\": {\"type\":\"object\",\"additionalProperties\":false,\"required\":[\"similarity_pct\",\"squared_distance_contribution\",\"reason\"],\"properties\":{\"similarity_pct\":{\"type\":[\"number\",\"null\"],\"minimum\":0,\"maximum\":100},\"squared_distance_contribution\":{\"type\":[\"number\",\"null\"],\"minimum\":0,\"maximum\":1},\"reason\":{\"type\":[\"string\",\"null\"],\"minLength\":1}},\"allOf\":[{\"if\":{\"properties\":{\"similarity_pct\":{\"type\":\"number\"}}},\"then\":{\"properties\":{\"squared_distance_contribution\":{\"type\":\"number\",\"minimum\":0,\"maximum\":1},\"reason\":{\"type\":\"null\"}}},\"else\":{\"properties\":{\"squared_distance_contribution\":{\"type\":\"null\"},\"reason\":{\"type\":\"string\",\"minLength\":1}}}}]},\n    \"breakdown\": {\"type\":\"object\",\"additionalProperties\":false,\"required\":[\"slope_mean_deg\",\"slope_max_deg\",\"roughness_index\",\"elevation_delta_m\",\"temp_min_c\",\"temp_max_c\",\"solar_irradiance_kwh_m2\",\"aridity_index\",\"albedo\"],\"properties\":{\"slope_mean_deg\":{\"$ref\":\"#/$defs/featureBreakdown\"},\"slope_max_deg\":{\"$ref\":\"#/$defs/featureBreakdown\"},\"roughness_index\":{\"$ref\":\"#/$defs/featureBreakdown\"},\"elevation_delta_m\":{\"$ref\":\"#/$defs/featureBreakdown\"},\"temp_min_c\":{\"$ref\":\"#/$defs/featureBreakdown\"},\"temp_max_c\":{\"$ref\":\"#/$defs/featureBreakdown\"},\"solar_irradiance_kwh_m2\":{\"$ref\":\"#/$defs/featureBreakdown\"},\"aridity_index\":{\"$ref\":\"#/$defs/featureBreakdown\"},\"albedo\":{\"$ref\":\"#/$defs/featureBreakdown\"}}},\n    \"analog\": {\"type\":\"object\",\"additionalProperties\":false,\"required\":[\"location\",\"similarity_score\",\"rank\",\"breakdown\",\"coverage\"],\"properties\":{\"location\":{\"allOf\":[{\"$ref\":\"#/$defs/location\"},{\"properties\":{\"body\":{\"const\":\"earth\"}}}]},\"similarity_score\":{\"type\":\"number\",\"minimum\":0,\"maximum\":100},\"rank\":{\"type\":\"integer\",\"minimum\":1,\"maximum\":3},\"breakdown\":{\"$ref\":\"#/$defs/breakdown\"},\"coverage\":{\"type\":\"number\",\"minimum\":0,\"maximum\":1}}},\n    \"excluded\": {\"type\":\"object\",\"additionalProperties\":false,\"required\":[\"location_id\",\"reasons\"],\"properties\":{\"location_id\":{\"type\":\"string\",\"minLength\":1},\"reasons\":{\"type\":\"array\",\"items\":{\"type\":\"string\",\"minLength\":1},\"uniqueItems\":true,\"minItems\":1}}},\n    \"profileFeature\": {\"type\":\"object\",\"additionalProperties\":false,\"required\":[\"key\",\"unit\",\"quantity\",\"weight\",\"lower\",\"upper\",\"calibration_source_ids\",\"calibration_method\"],\"properties\":{\"key\":{\"enum\":[\"slope_mean_deg\",\"slope_max_deg\",\"roughness_index\",\"elevation_delta_m\",\"temp_min_c\",\"temp_max_c\",\"solar_irradiance_kwh_m2\",\"aridity_index\",\"albedo\"]},\"unit\":{\"enum\":[\"deg\",\"1\",\"m\",\"degC\",\"kWh/m2/day\"]},\"quantity\":{\"type\":\"string\",\"minLength\":1},\"weight\":{\"type\":\"number\",\"exclusiveMinimum\":0},\"lower\":{\"type\":\"number\"},\"upper\":{\"type\":\"number\"},\"calibration_source_ids\":{\"type\":\"array\",\"items\":{\"type\":\"string\",\"minLength\":1},\"uniqueItems\":true,\"minItems\":1},\"calibration_method\":{\"type\":\"string\",\"minLength\":1}},\"allOf\":[{\"if\":{\"properties\":{\"key\":{\"const\":\"slope_mean_deg\"}}},\"then\":{\"properties\":{\"unit\":{\"const\":\"deg\"}}}},{\"if\":{\"properties\":{\"key\":{\"const\":\"slope_max_deg\"}}},\"then\":{\"properties\":{\"unit\":{\"const\":\"deg\"}}}},{\"if\":{\"properties\":{\"key\":{\"const\":\"roughness_index\"}}},\"then\":{\"properties\":{\"unit\":{\"const\":\"1\"}}}},{\"if\":{\"properties\":{\"key\":{\"const\":\"elevation_delta_m\"}}},\"then\":{\"properties\":{\"unit\":{\"const\":\"m\"}}}},{\"if\":{\"properties\":{\"key\":{\"const\":\"temp_min_c\"}}},\"then\":{\"properties\":{\"unit\":{\"const\":\"degC\"}}}},{\"if\":{\"properties\":{\"key\":{\"const\":\"temp_max_c\"}}},\"then\":{\"properties\":{\"unit\":{\"const\":\"degC\"}}}},{\"if\":{\"properties\":{\"key\":{\"const\":\"solar_irradiance_kwh_m2\"}}},\"then\":{\"properties\":{\"unit\":{\"const\":\"kWh/m2/day\"}}}},{\"if\":{\"properties\":{\"key\":{\"const\":\"aridity_index\"}}},\"then\":{\"properties\":{\"unit\":{\"const\":\"1\"}}}},{\"if\":{\"properties\":{\"key\":{\"const\":\"albedo\"}}},\"then\":{\"properties\":{\"unit\":{\"const\":\"1\"}}}}]},\n    \"scoringProfile\": {\"type\":\"object\",\"additionalProperties\":false,\"required\":[\"id\",\"version\",\"algorithm\",\"alpha\",\"features\",\"comparison_resolution_m\",\"window_m\",\"min_valid_area_fraction\"],\"properties\":{\"id\":{\"type\":\"string\",\"minLength\":1},\"version\":{\"type\":\"string\",\"minLength\":1},\"algorithm\":{\"const\":\"weighted_cosine_euclidean_v1\"},\"alpha\":{\"type\":\"number\",\"minimum\":0,\"maximum\":1},\"features\":{\"type\":\"array\",\"items\":{\"$ref\":\"#/$defs/profileFeature\"},\"minItems\":1,\"maxItems\":9,\"uniqueItems\":true},\"comparison_resolution_m\":{\"type\":\"number\",\"exclusiveMinimum\":0},\"window_m\":{\"type\":\"number\",\"exclusiveMinimum\":0},\"min_valid_area_fraction\":{\"type\":\"number\",\"exclusiveMinimum\":0,\"maximum\":1}}},\n    \"comparison\": {\"type\":\"object\",\"additionalProperties\":false,\"required\":[\"target\",\"profile_id\",\"analogs\",\"excluded\"],\"properties\":{\"target\":{\"allOf\":[{\"$ref\":\"#/$defs/location\"},{\"properties\":{\"body\":{\"enum\":[\"moon\",\"mars\"]}}}]},\"profile_id\":{\"type\":\"string\",\"minLength\":1},\"analogs\":{\"type\":\"array\",\"items\":{\"$ref\":\"#/$defs/analog\"},\"maxItems\":3},\"excluded\":{\"type\":\"array\",\"items\":{\"$ref\":\"#/$defs/excluded\"}}}}\n  }\n}\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "shared/types/analog-location.ts",
      "bytes": 5156,
      "sha256": "d072f39544a8c4c72f01459fa46fc7da070291e2f5b1cba06169833a7e9e3e06",
      "content": "/** Contract 1.0.0. Validate numeric bounds and cross-field invariants at runtime.\n * Heightmap invalid samples contain float 0 and mask 0; 0 is not a missing sentinel.\n */\nexport type Body = \"earth\" | \"moon\" | \"mars\";\nexport type FeatureKey =\n  | \"slope_mean_deg\" | \"slope_max_deg\" | \"roughness_index\"\n  | \"elevation_delta_m\" | \"temp_min_c\" | \"temp_max_c\"\n  | \"solar_irradiance_kwh_m2\" | \"aridity_index\" | \"albedo\";\nexport type FeatureUnit = \"deg\" | \"1\" | \"m\" | \"degC\" | \"kWh/m2/day\";\nexport type Vec3 = [number, number, number];\nexport type Mat4 = [number, number, number, number, number, number, number, number,\n  number, number, number, number, number, number, number, number];\nexport type LocalFrame = \"local_enu_x_east_y_up_z_south\";\nexport interface Source {\n  id: string; provider: string; product_id: string; product_url: string;\n  mission: string | null; access_url: string; retrieved_at: string;\n  sha256: string; license: string; local_path: string;\n}\nexport interface ObservationPeriod { start: string; end: string }\ninterface FeatureMetadata<U extends FeatureUnit> {\n  unit: U; quantity: string; method: string;\n  spatial_resolution_m: number | null; support_window_m: number | null;\n  period: ObservationPeriod | null;\n}\nexport type Feature<U extends FeatureUnit = FeatureUnit> = FeatureMetadata<U> & (\n  | { value: number; source_ids: [string, ...string[]]; missing_reason: null }\n  | { value: null; source_ids: string[]; missing_reason: string }\n);\nexport interface Features {\n  slope_mean_deg: Feature<\"deg\">; slope_max_deg: Feature<\"deg\">;\n  roughness_index: Feature<\"1\">; elevation_delta_m: Feature<\"m\">;\n  temp_min_c: Feature<\"degC\">; temp_max_c: Feature<\"degC\">;\n  solar_irradiance_kwh_m2: Feature<\"kWh/m2/day\">;\n  aridity_index: Feature<\"1\">; albedo: Feature<\"1\">;\n}\nexport type CoordinateFrame = \"earth_wgs84\" | \"moon_iau_planetocentric\" | \"mars_iau_planetocentric\";\nexport interface Coordinates {\n  latitude_deg: number; longitude_deg: number; frame: CoordinateFrame;\n  source_ids: [string, ...string[]];\n}\nexport interface AreaOfInterest { local_path: string; source_ids: [string, ...string[]] }\nexport interface Asset {\n  path: string; sha256: string; bytes: number; source_ids: [string, ...string[]];\n}\nexport interface Heightmap {\n  asset: Asset; valid_mask: Asset; width: number; height: number;\n  encoding: \"float32_le\"; nodata: null;\n  row_order: \"north_to_south\"; col_order: \"west_to_east\"; samples: \"grid_nodes\";\n  height_reference: \"offset_from_origin_elevation_m\";\n  extent_m: [number, number, number, number];\n  origin: { latitude_deg: number; longitude_deg: number; elevation_m: number };\n  frame: LocalFrame; vertical_datum: string; meters_per_unit: 1;\n}\nexport interface Splat {\n  asset: Asset; format: \"splat\" | \"ply\"; gaussian_count: number;\n  scale_is_metric: boolean; world_from_asset: Mat4; reconstruction_method: string;\n  geometry_provenance: \"photogrammetric\" | \"inferred\";\n}\nexport interface Camera {\n  position: Vec3; lookAt: Vec3; frame: LocalFrame; fov: number; near: number; far: number;\n}\nexport type Geology =\n  | { classes: [string, ...string[]]; method: string; source_ids: [string, ...string[]]; missing_reason: null }\n  | { classes: []; method: string; source_ids: string[]; missing_reason: string };\nexport interface Location<B extends Body = Body> {\n  id: string; name: string; body: B; coordinates: Coordinates;\n  aoi: AreaOfInterest; missions: string[];\n  features: Features; geology: Geology;\n  assets: { dem: Asset | null; heightmap: Heightmap | null; splat: Splat | null };\n  camera: Camera | null;\n}\nexport type FeatureBreakdown =\n  | { similarity_pct: number; squared_distance_contribution: number; reason: null }\n  | { similarity_pct: null; squared_distance_contribution: null; reason: string };\nexport interface Analog {\n  location: Location<\"earth\">; similarity_score: number; rank: 1 | 2 | 3;\n  breakdown: Record<FeatureKey, FeatureBreakdown>; coverage: number;\n}\nexport interface Excluded { location_id: string; reasons: [string, ...string[]] }\ntype ProfileFeatureBase<K extends FeatureKey, U extends FeatureUnit> = {\n  key: K; unit: U; quantity: string; weight: number; lower: number; upper: number;\n  calibration_source_ids: [string, ...string[]]; calibration_method: string;\n};\nexport type ProfileFeature =\n  | ProfileFeatureBase<\"slope_mean_deg\" | \"slope_max_deg\", \"deg\">\n  | ProfileFeatureBase<\"elevation_delta_m\", \"m\">\n  | ProfileFeatureBase<\"temp_min_c\" | \"temp_max_c\", \"degC\">\n  | ProfileFeatureBase<\"solar_irradiance_kwh_m2\", \"kWh/m2/day\">\n  | ProfileFeatureBase<\"roughness_index\" | \"aridity_index\" | \"albedo\", \"1\">;\nexport interface ScoringProfile {\n  id: string; version: string; algorithm: \"weighted_cosine_euclidean_v1\";\n  alpha: number; features: [ProfileFeature, ...ProfileFeature[]];\n  comparison_resolution_m: number; window_m: number; min_valid_area_fraction: number;\n}\nexport interface Comparison {\n  target: Location<\"moon\" | \"mars\">; profile_id: string;\n  analogs: Analog[]; excluded: Excluded[];\n}\nexport interface LocationsDataset {\n  schema_version: \"1.0.0\"; generated_at: string;\n  sources: Source[]; profiles: ScoringProfile[]; comparisons: Comparison[];\n}\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "src/App.tsx",
      "bytes": 16767,
      "sha256": "c67baa7655dc0d487529a8e2bea1e3067ca41687f25e5a4824a3c9332856b62e",
      "content": "import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';\nimport {\n  AlertCircle,\n  Download,\n  PanelRightClose,\n  PanelRightOpen,\n  RefreshCw,\n} from 'lucide-react';\nimport type { Body, Comparison, Location, LocationsDataset } from '@shared/types/analog-location';\nimport { TelemetryHUD } from './components/TelemetryHUD';\nimport { Viewport3D, type ViewportMode, type ViewportTelemetry } from './components/Viewport3D';\nimport { DatasetValidationError, loadDataset } from './data/loadDataset';\n\nconst EMPTY_DATASET: LocationsDataset = {\n  schema_version: '1.0.0',\n  generated_at: '1970-01-01T00:00:00Z',\n  sources: [],\n  profiles: [],\n  comparisons: [],\n};\n\nconst INITIAL_TELEMETRY: ViewportTelemetry = {\n  mode: 'orbit',\n  fps: null,\n  frameTimeMs: null,\n  dpr: 1,\n  isSoftwareGl: false,\n  localPosition_m: null,\n  absoluteElevation_m: null,\n  demSlopeDeg: null,\n  demTriangleCount: null,\n  verticalDatum: null,\n  verticalOrigin_m: null,\n  groundLoading: false,\n  groundError: null,\n  splatStatus: { status: 'none', message: '3D reconstruction not prepared' },\n  pointerLocked: false,\n  memoryUsedMiB: null,\n};\n\ninterface LocationOption {\n  location: Location;\n  comparison: Comparison;\n  label: string;\n}\n\nexport default function App(): React.JSX.Element {\n  const [dataset, setDataset] = useState<LocationsDataset>(EMPTY_DATASET);\n  const [loadState, setLoadState] = useState<'loading' | 'ready' | 'error'>('loading');\n  const [errorInfo, setErrorInfo] = useState<{\n    kind: string;\n    message: string;\n    details: string[];\n  } | null>(null);\n  const [reloadNonce, setReloadNonce] = useState(0);\n\n  const [activeBody, setActiveBody] = useState<Body>('moon');\n  const [selectedId, setSelectedId] = useState<string | null>(null);\n  const [mode, setMode] = useState<ViewportMode>('orbit');\n  const [walkSpeedMps, setWalkSpeedMps] = useState(3.5);\n  const [eyeHeightM, setEyeHeightM] = useState(1.6);\n  const [hudOpen, setHudOpen] = useState<boolean>(() =>\n    typeof window !== 'undefined' ? window.innerWidth >= 1024 : true,\n  );\n  const [telemetry, setTelemetry] = useState<ViewportTelemetry>(INITIAL_TELEMETRY);\n  const [devBundleMeta, setDevBundleMeta] = useState<{\n    available: boolean;\n    file_count: number;\n    bytes: number;\n    sha256: string;\n  } | null>(null);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (typeof window === 'undefined' || typeof window.matchMedia !== 'function') return;\n    const mq = window.matchMedia('(max-width: 1023px)');\n    const handleViewportChange = (ev: MediaQueryListEvent) => {\n      setHudOpen(!ev.matches);\n    };\n    mq.addEventListener('change', handleViewportChange);\n    return () => mq.removeEventListener('change', handleViewportChange);\n  }, []);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (!import.meta.env.DEV) return;\n    const controller = new AbortController();\n    fetch('/__dev/source-bundle-meta.json', { cache: 'no-store', signal: controller.signal })\n      .then(async (res) => {\n        if (!res.ok) return;\n        const json = (await res.json()) as {\n          available: boolean;\n          file_count: number;\n          bytes: number;\n          sha256: string;\n        };\n        if (!controller.signal.aborted) {\n          setDevBundleMeta(json);\n        }\n      })\n      .catch(() => {\n        // Optional dev metadata unavailable outside dev middleware\n      });\n    return () => controller.abort();\n  }, []);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const controller = new AbortController();\n    setLoadState('loading');\n    setErrorInfo(null);\n\n    loadDataset(controller.signal)\n      .then((loaded) => {\n        if (controller.signal.aborted) return;\n        setDataset(loaded);\n        setLoadState('ready');\n        if (loaded.comparisons.length > 0) {\n          const firstTarget = loaded.comparisons[0].target;\n          setSelectedId((prev) => prev ?? firstTarget.id);\n          setActiveBody(firstTarget.body);\n        } else {\n          setSelectedId(null);\n        }\n      })\n      .catch((err) => {\n        if (controller.signal.aborted) return;\n        if (err instanceof DOMException && err.name === 'AbortError') return;\n        setDataset(EMPTY_DATASET);\n        setSelectedId(null);\n        setLoadState('error');\n        if (err instanceof DatasetValidationError) {\n          setErrorInfo({\n            kind: err.kind,\n            message: err.message,\n            details: err.details.slice(0, 6),\n          });\n        } else {\n          setErrorInfo({\n            kind: 'unknown',\n            message: err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err),\n            details: [],\n          });\n        }\n      });\n\n    return () => {\n      controller.abort();\n    };\n  }, [reloadNonce]);\n\n  const locationOptions = useMemo<LocationOption[]>(() => {\n    const seen = new Set<string>();\n    const options: LocationOption[] = [];\n    for (const comp of dataset.comparisons) {\n      if (!seen.has(comp.target.id)) {\n        seen.add(comp.target.id);\n        options.push({\n          location: comp.target,\n          comparison: comp,\n          label: `${comp.target.body.toUpperCase()} · ${comp.target.name}`,\n        });\n      }\n      for (const analog of comp.analogs) {\n        if (!seen.has(analog.location.id)) {\n          seen.add(analog.location.id);\n          options.push({\n            location: analog.location,\n            comparison: comp,\n            label: `EARTH #${analog.rank} · ${analog.location.name} (${analog.similarity_score.toFixed(1)}%)`,\n          });\n        }\n      }\n    }\n    return options;\n  }, [dataset]);\n\n  const selectedEntry = useMemo(\n    () => locationOptions.find((opt) => opt.location.id === selectedId) ?? null,\n    [locationOptions, selectedId],\n  );\n\n  const handleTargetSelected = useCallback(\n    (id: string) => {\n      const match = locationOptions.find((opt) => opt.location.id === id);\n      setSelectedId(id);\n      if (match) {\n        setActiveBody(match.location.body);\n      }\n    },\n    [locationOptions],\n  );\n\n  const passportErrorMessage = useMemo(() => {\n    if (!errorInfo) return null;\n    const detailSuffix =\n      errorInfo.details.length > 0 ? ` (${errorInfo.details.join('; ')})` : '';\n    return `${errorInfo.kind}: ${errorInfo.message}${detailSuffix}`;\n  }, [errorInfo]);\n\n  const isPartialDataset =\n    loadState === 'ready' &&\n    (dataset.comparisons.length === 0 ||\n      dataset.comparisons.every((c) => c.analogs.length === 0));\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"flex h-screen w-screen flex-col overflow-hidden bg-[#07090E] text-slate-100\">\n      {/* Top Mission Control Header */}\n      <header className=\"flex min-h-12 shrink-0 flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-x-3 gap-y-1.5 border-b border-slate-800 bg-[#0B1018] px-3 py-1.5 sm:px-4\">\n        <div className=\"flex min-w-0 items-center gap-3\">\n          <h1 className=\"truncate text-sm font-semibold tracking-tight text-slate-100\">\n            Planetary Analog Explorer\n          </h1>\n          <span className=\"hidden md:inline text-xs text-slate-500 whitespace-nowrap\">\n            {loadState === 'ready'\n              ? `Dataset ${dataset.generated_at.slice(0, 10)} · ${dataset.comparisons.length} comparison${dataset.comparisons.length === 1 ? '' : 's'} · ${dataset.sources.length} source${dataset.sources.length === 1 ? '' : 's'}`\n              : loadState === 'loading'\n                ? 'Loading local dataset…'\n                : 'No active dataset'}\n          </span>\n        </div>\n\n        <div className=\"flex min-w-0 flex-wrap items-center gap-2\">\n          <label className=\"sr-only\" htmlFor=\"target-selector\">\n            Select target or analog site\n          </label>\n          <select\n            id=\"target-selector\"\n            data-testid=\"target-selector\"\n            value={selectedId ?? ''}\n            disabled={locationOptions.length === 0}\n            onChange={(e) => {\n              if (e.target.value) handleTargetSelected(e.target.value);\n            }}\n            className=\"h-8 min-w-0 max-w-[165px] border border-slate-700 bg-[#0F141E] px-2.5 text-xs text-slate-200 sm:max-w-[260px] disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:text-slate-500 focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-cyan-400\"\n          >\n            {locationOptions.length === 0 ? (\n              <option value=\"\">No prepared targets</option>\n            ) : (\n              locationOptions.map((opt) => (\n                <option key={opt.location.id} value={opt.location.id}>\n                  {opt.label}\n                </option>\n              ))\n            )}\n          </select>\n\n          <button\n            type=\"button\"\n            onClick={() => setReloadNonce((n) => n + 1)}\n            data-testid=\"reload-dataset-btn\"\n            className=\"inline-flex h-8 items-center gap-1.5 border border-slate-700 bg-[#0F141E] px-2.5 text-xs font-medium text-slate-200 hover:border-slate-600 hover:text-white whitespace-nowrap focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-cyan-400\"\n          >\n            <RefreshCw className=\"h-3.5 w-3.5\" />\n            <span>Reload</span>\n          </button>\n\n          {import.meta.env.DEV && (\n            <div\n              data-testid=\"dev-source-bundle-bar\"\n              className=\"flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 border border-cyan-500/40 bg-cyan-500/10 px-2 py-1 text-[11px] text-cyan-200\"\n            >\n              <a\n                href=\"/__dev/source-bundle.json\"\n                download=\"source-bundle.json\"\n                data-testid=\"download-source-bundle-link\"\n                className=\"inline-flex items-center gap-1 font-medium text-cyan-200 underline decoration-cyan-400/60 underline-offset-2 hover:text-white whitespace-nowrap focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-cyan-400\"\n              >\n                <Download className=\"h-3 w-3\" />\n                <span>Download source bundle</span>\n              </a>\n              <span aria-hidden=\"true\" className=\"text-cyan-500/60\">·</span>\n              <a\n                href=\"/__dev/source-bundle.json?view=1\"\n                target=\"_blank\"\n                rel=\"noopener noreferrer\"\n                data-testid=\"view-source-bundle-link\"\n                className=\"font-medium text-cyan-200 underline decoration-cyan-400/60 underline-offset-2 hover:text-white whitespace-nowrap focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-cyan-400\"\n              >\n                View JSON\n              </a>\n              {devBundleMeta?.available && (\n                <>\n                  <span aria-hidden=\"true\" className=\"text-cyan-500/60\">·</span>\n                  <span className=\"font-mono text-cyan-100 tabular-nums\">\n                    {devBundleMeta.file_count} files\n                  </span>\n                  <span aria-hidden=\"true\" className=\"text-cyan-500/60\">·</span>\n                  <code\n                    data-testid=\"dev-source-bundle-hash\"\n                    title={devBundleMeta.sha256}\n                    className=\"font-mono text-[10px] text-cyan-100\"\n                  >\n                    sha256:{devBundleMeta.sha256}\n                  </code>\n                </>\n              )}\n            </div>\n          )}\n\n          <button\n            type=\"button\"\n            onClick={() => setHudOpen((v) => !v)}\n            data-testid=\"toggle-telemetry-btn\"\n            aria-expanded={hudOpen}\n            aria-controls=\"telemetry-hud-panel\"\n            aria-label={hudOpen ? 'Collapse telemetry panel' : 'Expand telemetry panel'}\n            className=\"inline-flex h-8 items-center gap-1.5 border border-slate-700 bg-[#0F141E] px-2.5 text-xs font-medium text-slate-200 hover:border-slate-600 hover:text-white whitespace-nowrap focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-cyan-400\"\n          >\n            {hudOpen ? (\n              <PanelRightClose className=\"h-3.5 w-3.5\" />\n            ) : (\n              <PanelRightOpen className=\"h-3.5 w-3.5\" />\n            )}\n            <span>Telemetry</span>\n          </button>\n        </div>\n      </header>\n\n      {/* Honest Empty / Validation Error / Partial Dataset Banner */}\n      {loadState === 'error' && errorInfo && (\n        <div\n          role=\"alert\"\n          data-testid=\"dataset-error-banner\"\n          className=\"flex shrink-0 flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-3 border-b border-amber-500/40 bg-[#15120C] px-3 py-2.5 text-xs text-amber-200 sm:px-4\"\n        >\n          <div className=\"flex min-w-0 flex-1 items-start gap-2.5\">\n            <AlertCircle className=\"mt-0.5 h-4 w-4 shrink-0 text-amber-400\" />\n            <div className=\"min-w-0 flex-1\">\n              <div className=\"font-semibold text-amber-100\">\n                {errorInfo.kind === 'missing'\n                  ? 'Prepared Data Unavailable'\n                  : 'Dataset Validation Failed'}\n              </div>\n              <p className=\"mt-0.5 text-amber-200/90\">\n                {errorInfo.kind === 'missing'\n                  ? 'No prepared planetary or Earth analog site data is currently loaded in this viewer. Use Retry once prepared data is available.'\n                  : 'The dataset could not be loaded because it did not pass schema or scientific invariant checks.'}\n              </p>\n\n              <details\n                data-testid=\"technical-details-disclosure\"\n                className=\"mt-1.5 text-amber-200/80\"\n              >\n                <summary className=\"inline-flex cursor-pointer list-none items-center gap-1 font-medium text-amber-300 underline decoration-amber-500/50 underline-offset-2 hover:text-amber-100 focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-amber-400\">\n                  Technical details\n                </summary>\n                <div className=\"mt-1.5 border border-amber-500/25 bg-[#0B0E14] px-2.5 py-2 font-mono text-[11px] text-amber-200/90\">\n                  <div>Error kind: {errorInfo.kind}</div>\n                  <div className=\"mt-0.5 break-words\">{errorInfo.message}</div>\n                  {errorInfo.details.length > 0 && (\n                    <ul className=\"mt-1 list-disc pl-4\">\n                      {errorInfo.details.map((d, idx) => (\n                        <li key={idx} className=\"break-words\">\n                          {d}\n                        </li>\n                      ))}\n                    </ul>\n                  )}\n                </div>\n              </details>\n            </div>\n          </div>\n          <button\n            type=\"button\"\n            onClick={() => setReloadNonce((n) => n + 1)}\n            data-testid=\"retry-dataset-btn\"\n            className=\"inline-flex shrink-0 items-center gap-1.5 border border-amber-400/50 bg-amber-500/15 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-amber-100 hover:bg-amber-500/25 whitespace-nowrap focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-amber-400\"\n          >\n            <RefreshCw className=\"h-3.5 w-3.5\" />\n            <span>Retry</span>\n          </button>\n        </div>\n      )}\n\n      {isPartialDataset && (\n        <div\n          role=\"status\"\n          data-testid=\"dataset-partial-banner\"\n          className=\"shrink-0 border-b border-slate-800 bg-[#0F141E] px-4 py-2 text-xs text-slate-300\"\n        >\n          Dataset loaded with partial coverage: no ranked Earth analogs are present yet; showing\n          available prepared locations only.\n        </div>\n      )}\n\n      {/* Main Split Workspace: Viewport3D + Collapsible TelemetryHUD */}\n      <main className=\"flex min-h-0 flex-1 flex-col overflow-hidden lg:flex-row\">\n        <div className=\"relative min-h-[240px] min-w-0 flex-1\">\n          <Viewport3D\n            selected={selectedEntry?.location ?? null}\n            dataset={dataset}\n            onTargetSelected={handleTargetSelected}\n            activeBody={activeBody}\n            onBodyChange={setActiveBody}\n            mode={mode}\n            onModeChange={setMode}\n            walkSpeedMps={walkSpeedMps}\n            eyeHeightM={eyeHeightM}\n            onTelemetryUpdate={setTelemetry}\n          />\n        </div>\n\n        {hudOpen && (\n          <div\n            id=\"telemetry-hud-panel\"\n            data-testid=\"telemetry-hud-panel\"\n            className=\"h-[46vh] max-h-[420px] w-full shrink-0 border-t border-slate-800 lg:h-full lg:max-h-none lg:w-[400px] lg:border-t-0 lg:border-l\"\n          >\n            <TelemetryHUD\n              dataset={dataset}\n              selected={selectedEntry?.location ?? null}\n              comparison={selectedEntry?.comparison ?? null}\n              telemetry={telemetry}\n              walkSpeedMps={walkSpeedMps}\n              eyeHeightM={eyeHeightM}\n              onWalkSpeedChange={setWalkSpeedMps}\n              onEyeHeightChange={setEyeHeightM}\n              onSelectLocation={handleTargetSelected}\n              loading={loadState === 'loading'}\n              errorMessage={passportErrorMessage}\n            />\n          </div>\n        )}\n      </main>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "src/components/AnalogPassport.tsx",
      "bytes": 28583,
      "sha256": "5d8964e89882103362d4e20fdf59d7d94395965db90b96de4f1570013ab045df",
      "content": "import React, { useMemo } from 'react';\nimport type {\n  Analog,\n  Comparison,\n  Feature,\n  FeatureKey,\n  Location,\n  ScoringProfile,\n  Source,\n} from '@shared/types/analog-location';\nimport { InfoDisclosure } from './ui/InfoDisclosure';\n\nexport interface AnalogPassportProps {\n  comparison: Comparison | null;\n  selectedAnalog: Analog | null;\n  profile: ScoringProfile | null;\n  sources: readonly Source[];\n  loading: boolean;\n  errorMessage: string | null;\n}\n\nconst ALL_FEATURE_KEYS: readonly FeatureKey[] = [\n  'slope_mean_deg',\n  'slope_max_deg',\n  'roughness_index',\n  'elevation_delta_m',\n  'temp_min_c',\n  'temp_max_c',\n  'solar_irradiance_kwh_m2',\n  'aridity_index',\n  'albedo',\n] as const;\n\nconst FEATURE_LABELS: Record<FeatureKey, string> = {\n  slope_mean_deg: 'Mean slope',\n  slope_max_deg: 'Maximum slope',\n  roughness_index: 'Roughness index',\n  elevation_delta_m: 'Elevation relief',\n  temp_min_c: 'Minimum temperature',\n  temp_max_c: 'Maximum temperature',\n  solar_irradiance_kwh_m2: 'Daily solar irradiation (mean daily kWh/m²/day)',\n  aridity_index: 'Aridity index',\n  albedo: 'Surface albedo',\n};\n\nfunction formatNumericValue(value: number): string {\n  return Number.isInteger(value) ? String(value) : value.toFixed(2);\n}\n\nfunction formatUnitDisplay(key: FeatureKey, unit: Feature['unit']): string {\n  if (key === 'solar_irradiance_kwh_m2') {\n    return 'kWh/m²/day (mean daily)';\n  }\n  return unit;\n}\n\nfunction formatUtcTimestamp(iso: string): string {\n  if (!iso) return 'Not recorded';\n  return iso.length >= 19 ? `${iso.slice(0, 10)} ${iso.slice(11, 19)} UTC` : iso;\n}\n\nfunction formatBodyLabel(body: Location['body']): string {\n  if (body === 'moon') return 'Moon';\n  if (body === 'mars') return 'Mars';\n  return 'Earth';\n}\n\nfunction toSafeHttpsUrl(rawUrl: string): string | null {\n  try {\n    const parsed = new URL(rawUrl);\n    if (parsed.protocol !== 'https:') return null;\n    if (parsed.username !== '' || parsed.password !== '') return null;\n    return parsed.toString();\n  } catch {\n    return null;\n  }\n}\n\nfunction collectLocationSourceIds(location: Location, out: Set<string>): void {\n  for (const id of location.coordinates.source_ids) out.add(id);\n  for (const id of location.aoi.source_ids) out.add(id);\n  for (const id of location.geology.source_ids) out.add(id);\n\n  for (const key of ALL_FEATURE_KEYS) {\n    for (const id of location.features[key].source_ids) out.add(id);\n  }\n\n  if (location.assets.dem) {\n    for (const id of location.assets.dem.source_ids) out.add(id);\n  }\n  if (location.assets.heightmap) {\n    for (const id of location.assets.heightmap.asset.source_ids) out.add(id);\n    for (const id of location.assets.heightmap.valid_mask.source_ids) out.add(id);\n  }\n  if (location.assets.splat) {\n    for (const id of location.assets.splat.asset.source_ids) out.add(id);\n  }\n}\n\nfunction FeatureValueBlock({\n  siteLabel,\n  featureKey,\n  feature,\n}: {\n  siteLabel: string;\n  featureKey: FeatureKey;\n  feature: Feature;\n}): React.JSX.Element {\n  return (\n    <div className=\"mt-1.5 border-l-2 border-slate-700 pl-2 text-[11px]\">\n      <div className=\"flex flex-wrap items-baseline justify-between gap-2\">\n        <span className=\"font-medium text-slate-300\">{siteLabel}</span>\n        <span\n          className={`font-mono tabular-nums ${\n            feature.value !== null ? 'text-slate-100' : 'text-amber-300'\n          }`}\n        >\n          {feature.value !== null\n            ? `${formatNumericValue(feature.value)} ${formatUnitDisplay(featureKey, feature.unit)}`\n            : 'Not available'}\n        </span>\n      </div>\n      <div className=\"mt-0.5 text-slate-400\">\n        Quantity: {feature.quantity} · Method: {feature.method}\n      </div>\n      <div className=\"mt-0.5 font-mono text-slate-400 tabular-nums\">\n        Spatial resolution:{' '}\n        {feature.spatial_resolution_m !== null ? `${feature.spatial_resolution_m} m` : 'Not recorded'}{' '}\n        · Support window:{' '}\n        {feature.support_window_m !== null ? `${feature.support_window_m} m` : 'Not recorded'}\n      </div>\n      <div className=\"mt-0.5 font-mono text-slate-400 tabular-nums\">\n        Period:{' '}\n        {feature.period !== null\n          ? `${feature.period.start.slice(0, 10)} → ${feature.period.end.slice(0, 10)}`\n          : 'Not recorded'}\n      </div>\n      {feature.value === null && (\n        <div className=\"mt-0.5 text-amber-300\">Missing reason: {feature.missing_reason}</div>\n      )}\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\nexport function AnalogPassport({\n  comparison,\n  selectedAnalog,\n  profile,\n  sources,\n  loading,\n  errorMessage,\n}: AnalogPassportProps): React.JSX.Element {\n  const matchedAnalog = useMemo<Analog | null>(() => {\n    if (!comparison || !selectedAnalog) return null;\n    const found = comparison.analogs.find(\n      (entry) =>\n        entry === selectedAnalog ||\n        (entry.location.id === selectedAnalog.location.id &&\n          entry.rank === selectedAnalog.rank &&\n          entry.similarity_score === selectedAnalog.similarity_score),\n    );\n    return found ?? null;\n  }, [comparison, selectedAnalog]);\n\n  const analogBelongsToComparison =\n    selectedAnalog === null || (comparison !== null && matchedAnalog !== null);\n\n  const profileMatchesComparison =\n    comparison !== null && profile !== null && profile.id === comparison.profile_id;\n\n  const missingLimitItems = useMemo(() => {\n    if (!comparison) return [];\n    const items: Array<{ key: string; site: string; field: string; reason: string }> = [];\n\n    const inspectLocation = (loc: Location, roleLabel: string) => {\n      for (const fKey of ALL_FEATURE_KEYS) {\n        const feat = loc.features[fKey];\n        if (feat.value === null) {\n          items.push({\n            key: `${loc.id}:feature:${fKey}`,\n            site: `${roleLabel} (${loc.name})`,\n            field: FEATURE_LABELS[fKey],\n            reason: feat.missing_reason,\n          });\n        }\n      }\n      if (loc.geology.missing_reason !== null) {\n        items.push({\n          key: `${loc.id}:geology`,\n          site: `${roleLabel} (${loc.name})`,\n          field: 'Geology classification',\n          reason: loc.geology.missing_reason,\n        });\n      }\n    };\n\n    inspectLocation(comparison.target, 'Target');\n    if (matchedAnalog) {\n      inspectLocation(matchedAnalog.location, 'Analog');\n    }\n    return items;\n  }, [comparison, matchedAnalog]);\n\n  const inactiveFeatureKeys = useMemo<FeatureKey[]>(() => {\n    if (!profileMatchesComparison || !profile) return [];\n    const activeSet = new Set<FeatureKey>(profile.features.map((f) => f.key));\n    return ALL_FEATURE_KEYS.filter((k) => !activeSet.has(k));\n  }, [profileMatchesComparison, profile]);\n\n  const referencedSources = useMemo(() => {\n    if (!comparison) return [];\n    const ids = new Set<string>();\n    collectLocationSourceIds(comparison.target, ids);\n    if (matchedAnalog) {\n      collectLocationSourceIds(matchedAnalog.location, ids);\n    }\n    if (profileMatchesComparison && profile) {\n      for (const pf of profile.features) {\n        for (const id of pf.calibration_source_ids) {\n          ids.add(id);\n        }\n      }\n    }\n\n    const byId = new Map<string, Source>();\n    for (const src of sources) {\n      byId.set(src.id, src);\n    }\n\n    return Array.from(ids).map((id) => ({\n      id,\n      source: byId.get(id) ?? null,\n    }));\n  }, [comparison, matchedAnalog, profileMatchesComparison, profile, sources]);\n\n  const terrainLimitSites = useMemo(() => {\n    if (!comparison) return [];\n    const list: Array<{ key: string; role: string; location: Location }> = [\n      { key: `target:${comparison.target.id}`, role: 'Target', location: comparison.target },\n    ];\n    if (matchedAnalog) {\n      list.push({\n        key: `analog:${matchedAnalog.location.id}`,\n        role: 'Selected analog',\n        location: matchedAnalog.location,\n      });\n    }\n    return list;\n  }, [comparison, matchedAnalog]);\n\n  return (\n    <section\n      data-testid=\"analog-passport\"\n      aria-label=\"Analog Passport\"\n      className=\"space-y-3 p-4\"\n    >\n      <div>\n        <h3 className=\"text-sm font-semibold tracking-tight text-slate-100\">\n          Analog Passport\n        </h3>\n        <p className=\"mt-0.5 text-[11px] text-slate-400\">\n          Inspect the evidence behind a comparison\n        </p>\n      </div>\n\n      {loading && (\n        <div\n          role=\"status\"\n          data-testid=\"passport-loading-status\"\n          className=\"border border-cyan-500/30 bg-cyan-500/10 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-cyan-200\"\n        >\n          Loading prepared comparison dataset…\n        </div>\n      )}\n\n      {errorMessage && (\n        <div\n          role=\"status\"\n          data-testid=\"passport-error-status\"\n          className=\"space-y-2 border border-amber-500/40 bg-[#15120C] p-3 text-xs text-amber-200\"\n
