  >\n          <div className=\"font-medium text-amber-100\">\n            Dataset error: comparison evidence could not be loaded.\n          </div>\n          <InfoDisclosure label=\"Technical details\">\n            <div className=\"break-words font-mono text-[11px] text-amber-200/90\">\n              {errorMessage}\n            </div>\n          </InfoDisclosure>\n        </div>\n      )}\n\n      {comparison === null && !loading && (\n        <div\n          data-testid=\"passport-empty-state\"\n          className=\"border border-slate-800 bg-[#0F141E] px-3 py-2.5 text-xs leading-relaxed text-slate-300\"\n        >\n          <div className=\"font-medium text-slate-100\">No prepared comparison yet</div>\n          <p className=\"mt-1 text-slate-400\">\n            A comparison will appear once a validated source-backed dataset is loaded.\n          </p>\n        </div>\n      )}\n\n      {comparison !== null && !analogBelongsToComparison && (\n        <div\n          role=\"status\"\n          data-testid=\"passport-analog-mismatch\"\n          className=\"border border-amber-500/40 bg-[#15120C] px-3 py-2 text-xs text-amber-200\"\n        >\n          Selected analog does not belong to the active comparison ({comparison.target.name}); match\n          analytics are unavailable.\n        </div>\n      )}\n\n      <div className=\"space-y-2\" data-testid=\"passport-disclosures\">\n        {/* 1. Physical profile */}\n        <InfoDisclosure label=\"Physical profile\">\n          {comparison === null ? (\n            <p className=\"text-slate-400\">\n              No prepared comparison yet. Physical measurements, units, resolution, and geology\n              classes will appear once a dataset is loaded.\n            </p>\n          ) : (\n            <div className=\"space-y-3\">\n              <div className=\"border-b border-slate-800 pb-2 text-[11px]\">\n                <div>\n                  <span className=\"text-slate-400\">Target: </span>\n                  <span className=\"font-semibold text-slate-100\">{comparison.target.name}</span>{' '}\n                  <span className=\"text-cyan-300\">\n                    ({formatBodyLabel(comparison.target.body)})\n                  </span>\n                </div>\n                {matchedAnalog ? (\n                  <div className=\"mt-1\">\n                    <span className=\"text-slate-400\">Selected Earth analog: </span>\n                    <span className=\"font-semibold text-slate-100\">\n                      {matchedAnalog.location.name}\n                    </span>{' '}\n                    <span className=\"font-mono text-cyan-300 tabular-nums\">\n                      (#{matchedAnalog.rank})\n                    </span>\n                  </div>\n                ) : (\n                  <div className=\"mt-1 text-slate-400\">\n                    {selectedAnalog !== null\n                      ? 'Selected analog is unavailable for this comparison; showing target measurements only.'\n                      : 'No Earth analog selected; showing target measurements only.'}\n                  </div>\n                )}\n              </div>\n\n              <div className=\"space-y-2\">\n                {ALL_FEATURE_KEYS.map((key) => {\n                  const targetFeature = comparison.target.features[key];\n                  const analogFeature = matchedAnalog?.location.features[key] ?? null;\n                  return (\n                    <div\n                      key={`${comparison.target.id}:${key}`}\n                      className=\"border border-slate-800/80 bg-[#0B1018] p-2\"\n                    >\n                      <div className=\"text-xs font-medium text-slate-100\">\n                        {FEATURE_LABELS[key]}\n                      </div>\n                      <FeatureValueBlock\n                        siteLabel={`Target · ${comparison.target.name}`}\n                        featureKey={key}\n                        feature={targetFeature}\n                      />\n                      {matchedAnalog && analogFeature && (\n                        <FeatureValueBlock\n                          siteLabel={`Analog · ${matchedAnalog.location.name}`}\n                          featureKey={key}\n                          feature={analogFeature}\n                        />\n                      )}\n                    </div>\n                  );\n                })}\n              </div>\n\n              <div className=\"border border-slate-800/80 bg-[#0B1018] p-2 text-[11px]\">\n                <div className=\"font-medium text-slate-100\">Geology (categorical)</div>\n                <div className=\"mt-1 text-slate-300\">\n                  <span className=\"text-slate-400\">Target ({comparison.target.name}): </span>\n                  {comparison.target.geology.classes.length > 0\n                    ? comparison.target.geology.classes.join(', ')\n                    : `Not available (${comparison.target.geology.missing_reason})`}\n                  <div className=\"text-slate-400\">Method: {comparison.target.geology.method}</div>\n                </div>\n                {matchedAnalog && (\n                  <div className=\"mt-2 border-t border-slate-800 pt-1.5 text-slate-300\">\n                    <span className=\"text-slate-400\">\n                      Analog ({matchedAnalog.location.name}):{' '}\n                    </span>\n                    {matchedAnalog.location.geology.classes.length > 0\n                      ? matchedAnalog.location.geology.classes.join(', ')\n                      : `Not available (${matchedAnalog.location.geology.missing_reason})`}\n                    <div className=\"text-slate-400\">\n                      Method: {matchedAnalog.location.geology.method}\n                    </div>\n                  </div>\n                )}\n              </div>\n            </div>\n          )}\n        </InfoDisclosure>\n\n        {/* 2. Why this match */}\n        <InfoDisclosure label=\"Why this match\">\n          {comparison === null ? (\n            <p className=\"text-slate-400\">\n              No prepared comparison yet. Project-defined similarity scores and per-feature\n              breakdown require a loaded comparison and Earth analog.\n            </p>\n          ) : selectedAnalog === null ? (\n            <p className=\"text-slate-400\">\n              Select an Earth analog in this comparison to inspect why it ranked as a match.\n            </p>\n          ) : !matchedAnalog ? (\n            <p className=\"text-amber-300\">\n              Match breakdown unavailable: the selected analog does not belong to{' '}\n              {comparison.target.name}.\n            </p>\n          ) : profile === null ? (\n            <p className=\"text-amber-300\">\n              Match breakdown unavailable: scoring profile {comparison.profile_id} is missing from\n              the dataset.\n            </p>\n          ) : !profileMatchesComparison ? (\n            <p className=\"text-amber-300\">\n              Match breakdown unavailable: scoring profile mismatch (comparison expects{' '}\n              {comparison.profile_id}, provided {profile.id}).\n            </p>\n          ) : (\n            <div className=\"space-y-3 text-[11px]\">\n              <div className=\"border border-slate-800/80 bg-[#0B1018] p-2.5\">\n                <div className=\"flex items-baseline justify-between gap-2\">\n                  <span className=\"font-medium text-slate-200\">\n                    Project-defined similarity index (#{matchedAnalog.rank}{' '}\n                    {matchedAnalog.location.name})\n                  </span>\n                  <span className=\"font-mono text-sm font-semibold text-cyan-300 tabular-nums\">\n                    {matchedAnalog.similarity_score.toFixed(1)}%\n                  </span>\n                </div>\n                <p className=\"mt-1 text-slate-400\">\n                  Computed by profile{' '}\n                  <span className=\"font-mono text-slate-200\">\n                    {profile.id} v{profile.version}\n                  </span>{' '}\n                  ({profile.algorithm}, {profile.features.length} active features). This is a\n                  project-defined similarity index, not habitability probability or mission\n                  certification.\n                </p>\n\n                <div className=\"mt-2 flex items-baseline justify-between gap-2 border-t border-slate-800 pt-2\">\n                  <span className=\"font-medium text-slate-200\">Active-profile coverage</span>\n                  <span className=\"font-mono text-slate-100 tabular-nums\">\n                    {(matchedAnalog.coverage * 100).toFixed(0)}%\n                  </span>\n                </div>\n                <p className=\"mt-0.5 text-slate-400\">\n                  Fraction of active scoring-profile features with non-null observations on both\n                  sites; it does not cover unmodeled environmental physics.\n                </p>\n              </div>\n\n              <div className=\"space-y-1.5\">\n                <div className=\"font-medium text-slate-200\">\n                  Active profile feature contributions ({profile.features.length})\n                </div>\n                {profile.features.map((pf) => {\n                  const bd = matchedAnalog.breakdown[pf.key];\n                  return (\n                    <div\n                      key={`${profile.id}:${pf.key}`}\n                      className=\"border border-slate-800/80 bg-[#0B1018] p-2\"\n                    >\n                      <div className=\"flex flex-wrap items-baseline justify-between gap-2\">\n                        <span className=\"font-medium text-slate-200\">\n                          {FEATURE_LABELS[pf.key]}\n                        </span>\n                        <span className=\"font-mono text-slate-400 tabular-nums\">\n                          weight {pf.weight} · [{pf.lower}, {pf.upper}] {pf.unit}\n                        </span>\n                      </div>\n                      {bd.similarity_pct !== null ? (\n                        <div className=\"mt-1 space-y-0.5 font-mono text-cyan-300 tabular-nums\">\n                          <div>Per-feature similarity: {bd.similarity_pct.toFixed(1)}%</div>\n                          <div className=\"text-slate-300\">\n                            Normalized Euclidean squared distance contribution:{' '}\n                            {bd.squared_distance_contribution.toFixed(4)}\n                          </div>\n                          <div className=\"font-sans text-slate-400\">\n                            Distance term only; not a percentage share of the total hybrid score.\n                          </div>\n                        </div>\n                      ) : (\n                        <div className=\"mt-1 text-amber-300\">Omitted: {bd.reason}</div>\n                      )}\n                    </div>\n                  );\n                })}\n              </div>\n            </div>\n          )}\n        </InfoDisclosure>\n\n        {/* 3. Limits of this comparison */}\n        <InfoDisclosure label=\"Limits of this comparison\">\n          {comparison === null ? (\n            <p className=\"text-slate-400\">\n              No prepared comparison yet. Missing observations, inactive features, excluded\n              candidates, and terrain reconstruction caveats will appear here once loaded.\n            </p>\n          ) : (\n            <div className=\"space-y-3 text-[11px]\">\n              <div>\n                <div className=\"font-medium text-slate-200\">Missing measurements &amp; geology</div>\n                {missingLimitItems.length === 0 ? (\n                  <p className=\"mt-1 text-slate-400\">\n                    All 9 numeric features and categorical geology classes are recorded for the\n                    inspected site(s).\n                  </p>\n                ) : (\n                  <ul className=\"mt-1 space-y-1\">\n                    {missingLimitItems.map((item) => (\n                      <li\n                        key={item.key}\n                        className=\"border border-slate-800/80 bg-[#0B1018] px-2 py-1.5 text-slate-300\"\n                      >\n                        <span className=\"font-medium text-slate-200\">\n                          {item.site} · {item.field}:\n                        </span>{' '}\n                        <span className=\"text-amber-300\">{item.reason}</span>\n                      </li>\n                    ))}\n                  </ul>\n                )}\n              </div>\n\n              <div>\n                <div className=\"font-medium text-slate-200\">\n                  Inactive numeric features vs active profile\n                </div>\n                {!profileMatchesComparison ? (\n                  <p className=\"mt-1 text-amber-300\">\n                    Unavailable: matching scoring profile ({comparison.profile_id}) not loaded.\n                  </p>\n                ) : inactiveFeatureKeys.length === 0 ? (\n                  <p className=\"mt-1 text-slate-400\">\n                    All 9 schema numeric features are active in profile {profile?.id}.\n                  </p>\n                ) : (\n                  <ul className=\"mt-1 list-disc space-y-0.5 pl-4 text-slate-300\">\n                    {inactiveFeatureKeys.map((k) => (\n                      <li key={`${comparison.profile_id}:inactive:${k}`}>\n                        <span className=\"font-mono text-slate-200\">{k}</span> ({FEATURE_LABELS[k]})\n                      </li>\n                    ))}\n                  </ul>\n                )}\n              </div>\n\n              <div>\n                <div className=\"font-medium text-slate-200\">\n                  Excluded candidates ({comparison.excluded.length})\n                </div>\n                {comparison.excluded.length === 0 ? (\n                  <p className=\"mt-1 text-slate-400\">\n                    No excluded Earth candidates recorded for {comparison.target.name}.\n                  </p>\n                ) : (\n                  <ul className=\"mt-1 space-y-1\">\n                    {comparison.excluded.map((ex) => (\n                      <li\n                        key={`${comparison.target.id}:excluded:${ex.location_id}`}\n                        className=\"border border-slate-800/80 bg-[#0B1018] px-2 py-1.5 text-slate-300\"\n                      >\n                        <span className=\"font-mono text-slate-200\">{ex.location_id}</span>:{' '}\n                        {ex.reasons.join('; ')}\n                      </li>\n                    ))}\n                  </ul>\n                )}\n              </div>\n\n              <div>\n                <div className=\"font-medium text-slate-200\">\n                  Terrain DEM &amp; 3D reconstruction limits\n                </div>\n                <div className=\"mt-1 space-y-1.5\">\n                  {terrainLimitSites.map(({ key, role, location }) => {\n                    const hm = location.assets.heightmap;\n                    const splat = location.assets.splat;\n                    return (\n                      <div\n                        key={key}\n                        className=\"border border-slate-800/80 bg-[#0B1018] px-2 py-1.5 text-slate-300\"\n                      >\n                        <div className=\"font-medium text-slate-200\">\n                          {role}: {location.name}\n                        </div>\n                        <div className=\"mt-0.5\">\n                          Heightmap:{' '}\n                          {hm\n                            ? `${hm.width}×${hm.height} grid (${hm.vertical_datum})`\n                            : 'Not prepared (null) — 3D ground terrain view unavailable.'}\n                        </div>\n                        <div className=\"mt-0.5\">\n                          3D Splat:{' '}\n                          {splat === null\n                            ? 'Not prepared (null).'\n                            : `${splat.gaussian_count.toLocaleString()} Gaussians (${splat.geometry_provenance}, scale_is_metric=${String(splat.scale_is_metric)}).`}\n                        </div>\n                        {splat && (!splat.scale_is_metric || splat.geometry_provenance === 'inferred') && (\n                          <div className=\"mt-0.5 text-amber-300\">\n                            Caveat: inferred or non-metric splat geometry is not a metric terrain\n                            measurement.\n                          </div>\n                        )}\n                      </div>\n                    );\n                  })}\n                </div>\n              </div>\n            </div>\n          )}\n        </InfoDisclosure>\n\n        {/* 4. Source evidence */}\n        <InfoDisclosure label=\"Source evidence\">\n          {comparison === null || referencedSources.length === 0 ? (\n            <p className=\"text-slate-400\">\n              No referenced source records yet. Citations and recorded checksums will appear when\n              a source-backed comparison is loaded.\n            </p>\n          ) : (\n            <ul className=\"space-y-2 text-[11px]\">\n              {referencedSources.map(({ id, source }) => {\n                if (!source) {\n                  return (\n                    <li\n                      key={id}\n                      className=\"border border-amber-500/30 bg-[#15120C] p-2 text-amber-200\"\n                    >\n                      <span className=\"font-mono\">{id}</span> — Unavailable (source metadata missing\n                      from dataset.sources)\n                    </li>\n                  );\n                }\n                const safeProductUrl = toSafeHttpsUrl(source.product_url);\n                return (\n                  <li\n                    key={source.id}\n                    className=\"border border-slate-800/80 bg-[#0B1018] p-2 text-slate-300\"\n                  >\n                    <div className=\"flex flex-wrap items-baseline justify-between gap-2\">\n                      <span className=\"font-semibold text-slate-100\">{source.provider}</span>\n                      <span className=\"font-mono text-slate-400\">{source.license}</span>\n                    </div>\n                    <div className=\"mt-0.5 font-mono text-slate-200\">\n                      ID: {source.id} · {source.product_id}\n                    </div>\n                    <div className=\"mt-0.5 text-slate-400\">\n                      Mission: {source.mission ?? 'Not recorded'} · Retrieved:{' '}\n                      <span className=\"font-mono tabular-nums\">\n                        {formatUtcTimestamp(source.retrieved_at)}\n                      </span>\n                    </div>\n                    <div className=\"mt-0.5 break-all font-mono text-slate-400\">\n                      Recorded checksum (sha256):{' '}\n                      <span className=\"text-slate-200\">{source.sha256}</span>\n                    </div>\n                    <div className=\"mt-0.5 break-all text-slate-400\">\n                      Local dataset path: <code className=\"font-mono text-slate-300\">{source.local_path}</code>\n                    </div>\n                    <div className=\"mt-1 border-t border-slate-800 pt-1\">\n                      {safeProductUrl ? (\n                        <a\n                          href={safeProductUrl}\n                          target=\"_blank\"\n                          rel=\"noopener noreferrer\"\n                          className=\"text-cyan-400 underline decoration-cyan-500/50 underline-offset-2 hover:text-cyan-300\"\n                        >\n                          Product reference (HTTPS)\n                        </a>\n                      ) : (\n                        <span className=\"text-amber-300\">\n                          Product reference link withheld (requires HTTPS URL without credentials)\n                        </span>\n                      )}\n                    </div>\n                  </li>\n                );\n              })}\n            </ul>\n          )}\n        </InfoDisclosure>\n      </div>\n    </section>\n  );\n}\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "src/components/TelemetryHUD.tsx",
      "bytes": 12160,
      "sha256": "7100f941b237963c460f911b76f729c147d91fc6137855d2deb0ad852a9f3963",
      "content": "import React, { useMemo } from 'react';\nimport {\n  Activity,\n  FlaskConical,\n  Layers,\n  SlidersHorizontal,\n} from 'lucide-react';\nimport type {\n  Analog,\n  Comparison,\n  Location,\n  LocationsDataset,\n} from '@shared/types/analog-location';\nimport { formatBodyName, formatCoordinateFrame } from '../terrain/coordinates';\nimport { AnalogPassport } from './AnalogPassport';\nimport type { ViewportTelemetry } from './Viewport3D';\n\nexport interface TelemetryHUDProps {\n  dataset: LocationsDataset;\n  selected: Location | null;\n  comparison: Comparison | null;\n  telemetry: ViewportTelemetry;\n  walkSpeedMps: number;\n  eyeHeightM: number;\n  onWalkSpeedChange: (speed: number) => void;\n  onEyeHeightChange: (height: number) => void;\n  onSelectLocation: (id: string) => void;\n  loading?: boolean;\n  errorMessage?: string | null;\n}\n\nexport function TelemetryHUD({\n  dataset,\n  selected,\n  comparison,\n  telemetry,\n  walkSpeedMps,\n  eyeHeightM,\n  onWalkSpeedChange,\n  onEyeHeightChange,\n  onSelectLocation,\n  loading = false,\n  errorMessage = null,\n}: TelemetryHUDProps): React.JSX.Element {\n  const profile = useMemo(\n    () =>\n      comparison\n        ? dataset.profiles.find((p) => p.id === comparison.profile_id) ?? null\n        : null,\n    [comparison, dataset.profiles],\n  );\n\n  const activeAnalogEntry: Analog | null = useMemo(\n    () =>\n      selected && comparison\n        ? comparison.analogs.find((a) => a.location.id === selected.id) ?? null\n        : null,\n    [selected, comparison],\n  );\n\n  return (\n    <aside\n      aria-label=\"Diagnostics, navigation preferences, and scientific provenance\"\n      className=\"mission-scroll flex h-full w-full flex-col divide-y divide-slate-800/90 overflow-y-auto bg-[#0B1018] text-slate-200\"\n    >\n      {/* Group 1: Measured Client Diagnostics */}\n      <section data-testid=\"hud-group-diagnostics\" className=\"p-4\">\n        <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between gap-2\">\n          <h2 className=\"flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-semibold tracking-wide text-slate-200\">\n            <Activity className=\"h-3.5 w-3.5 text-cyan-400\" />\n            <span>Measured Client Diagnostics</span>\n          </h2>\n          <span className=\"font-mono text-[11px] text-cyan-300 tabular-nums\">\n            {telemetry.mode === 'ground' ? 'Ground ENU' : 'Orbit Schematic'}\n          </span>\n        </div>\n        <p className=\"mt-0.5 text-[11px] text-slate-500\">\n          Browser WebGL measurements only; unmeasured values remain N/A.\n        </p>\n\n        <dl className=\"mt-3 grid grid-cols-2 gap-x-4 gap-y-2.5 text-xs\">\n          <div>\n            <dt className=\"text-slate-500\">Frame Rate / Time</dt>\n            <dd className=\"mt-0.5 font-mono text-slate-100 tabular-nums\">\n              {telemetry.fps !== null && telemetry.frameTimeMs !== null\n                ? `${telemetry.fps.toFixed(1)} FPS · ${telemetry.frameTimeMs.toFixed(1)} ms`\n                : 'Not measured (on-demand idle)'}\n            </dd>\n          </div>\n          <div>\n            <dt className=\"text-slate-500\">Pixel Ratio / GL</dt>\n            <dd className=\"mt-0.5 font-mono text-slate-100 tabular-nums\">\n              {telemetry.dpr.toFixed(2)}x · {telemetry.isSoftwareGl ? 'Software GL' : 'Hardware GL'}\n            </dd>\n          </div>\n          <div>\n            <dt className=\"text-slate-500\">Local ENU (X, Y, Z m)</dt>\n            <dd className=\"mt-0.5 font-mono text-slate-100 tabular-nums\">\n              {telemetry.localPosition_m\n                ? `${telemetry.localPosition_m[0].toFixed(1)}, ${telemetry.localPosition_m[1].toFixed(1)}, ${telemetry.localPosition_m[2].toFixed(1)}`\n                : 'N/A'}\n            </dd>\n          </div>\n          <div>\n            <dt className=\"text-slate-500\">Datum Elevation</dt>\n            <dd className=\"mt-0.5 font-mono text-slate-100 tabular-nums\">\n              {telemetry.absoluteElevation_m !== null\n                ? `${telemetry.absoluteElevation_m.toFixed(2)} m (${telemetry.verticalDatum ?? 'datum'})`\n                : 'N/A'}\n            </dd>\n          </div>\n          <div>\n            <dt className=\"text-slate-500\">DEM Slope (derived)</dt>\n            <dd className=\"mt-0.5 font-mono text-slate-100 tabular-nums\">\n              {telemetry.demSlopeDeg !== null ? `${telemetry.demSlopeDeg.toFixed(1)}°` : 'N/A'}\n            </dd>\n          </div>\n          <div>\n            <dt className=\"text-slate-500\">Mesh / VRAM</dt>\n            <dd className=\"mt-0.5 font-mono text-slate-100 tabular-nums\">\n              {telemetry.demTriangleCount !== null\n                ? `${telemetry.demTriangleCount.toLocaleString()} tris`\n                : 'N/A'}{' '}\n              · VRAM N/A\n            </dd>\n          </div>\n          <div>\n            <dt className=\"text-slate-500\">Sun Azimuth / Horizon</dt>\n            <dd className=\"mt-0.5 font-mono text-slate-400\">N/A (no ephemeris)</dd>\n          </div>\n          <div>\n            <dt className=\"text-slate-500\">JS Heap (optional)</dt>\n            <dd className=\"mt-0.5 font-mono text-slate-400 tabular-nums\">\n              {telemetry.memoryUsedMiB !== null ? `${telemetry.memoryUsedMiB.toFixed(1)} MiB` : 'N/A'}\n            </dd>\n          </div>\n        </dl>\n      </section>\n\n      {/* Group 2: Viewer Navigation Preferences */}\n      <section data-testid=\"hud-group-preferences\" className=\"p-4\">\n        <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between gap-2\">\n          <h2 className=\"flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-semibold tracking-wide text-slate-200\">\n            <SlidersHorizontal className=\"h-3.5 w-3.5 text-slate-400\" />\n            <span>Viewer Navigation Preferences</span>\n          </h2>\n          <span className=\"text-[11px] text-slate-500\">Non-scientific UI controls</span>\n        </div>\n        <div className=\"mt-3 grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 text-xs\">\n          <label className=\"flex flex-col gap-1\">\n            <span className=\"flex justify-between text-slate-400\">\n              <span>Walk Speed</span>\n              <span className=\"font-mono text-slate-200 tabular-nums\">{walkSpeedMps.toFixed(1)} m/s</span>\n            </span>\n            <input\n              type=\"range\"\n              min={0.5}\n              max={15}\n              step={0.5}\n              value={walkSpeedMps}\n              onChange={(e) => onWalkSpeedChange(Number(e.target.value))}\n              className=\"accent-cyan-400\"\n            />\n          </label>\n          <label className=\"flex flex-col gap-1\">\n            <span className=\"flex justify-between text-slate-400\">\n              <span>Eye Height</span>\n              <span className=\"font-mono text-slate-200 tabular-nums\">{eyeHeightM.toFixed(2)} m</span>\n            </span>\n            <input\n              type=\"range\"\n              min={0.5}\n              max={3.5}\n              step={0.1}\n              value={eyeHeightM}\n              onChange={(e) => onEyeHeightChange(Number(e.target.value))}\n              className=\"accent-cyan-400\"\n            />\n          </label>\n        </div>\n      </section>\n\n      {/* Group 3: Scientific Data & Source Provenance */}\n      <div data-testid=\"hud-group-scientific\" className=\"divide-y divide-slate-800/90\">\n        <div className=\"bg-[#090D14] px-4 py-2.5\">\n          <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between gap-2\">\n            <h2 className=\"flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-semibold tracking-wide text-slate-200\">\n              <FlaskConical className=\"h-3.5 w-3.5 text-amber-400\" />\n              <span>Scientific Data &amp; Provenance</span>\n            </h2>\n            <span className=\"text-[11px] text-slate-500\">\n              {selected ? 'Dataset-derived' : 'No data loaded'}\n            </span>\n          </div>\n        </div>\n\n        {selected && (\n          <section className=\"p-4\">\n            <div className=\"text-xs text-cyan-400\">\n              {formatBodyName(selected.body)} · {selected.id}\n            </div>\n            <h3 className=\"mt-0.5 text-base font-semibold text-slate-100\">{selected.name}</h3>\n            <dl className=\"mt-2.5 space-y-1 text-xs\">\n              <div className=\"flex justify-between gap-2\">\n                <dt className=\"text-slate-500\">Coordinates</dt>\n                <dd className=\"font-mono text-slate-200 tabular-nums\">\n                  {selected.coordinates.latitude_deg.toFixed(4)}°,{' '}\n                  {selected.coordinates.longitude_deg.toFixed(4)}°\n                </dd>\n              </div>\n              <div className=\"flex justify-between gap-2\">\n                <dt className=\"text-slate-500\">Reference Frame</dt>\n                <dd className=\"text-right text-slate-300\">\n                  {formatCoordinateFrame(selected.coordinates.frame)}\n                </dd>\n              </div>\n              <div className=\"flex justify-between gap-2\">\n                <dt className=\"text-slate-500\">Missions</dt>\n                <dd className=\"text-right text-slate-300\">\n                  {selected.missions.length > 0 ? selected.missions.join(', ') : 'None listed'}\n                </dd>\n              </div>\n            </dl>\n          </section>\n        )}\n\n        {comparison && (\n          <section className=\"p-4\">\n            <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between gap-2\">\n              <h3 className=\"flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-semibold tracking-wide text-slate-300\">\n                <Layers className=\"h-3.5 w-3.5\" />\n                <span>Comparison Sites</span>\n              </h3>\n              {profile && (\n                <span className=\"font-mono text-[11px] text-slate-400 tabular-nums\">\n                  {profile.id} v{profile.version} ({profile.features.length}/9)\n                </span>\n              )}\n            </div>\n\n            <div className=\"mt-2 space-y-1.5\">\n              <button\n                type=\"button\"\n                onClick={() => onSelectLocation(comparison.target.id)}\n                className={`w-full border px-2.5 py-1.5 text-left text-xs transition-colors focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-cyan-400 ${\n                  selected?.id === comparison.target.id\n                    ? 'border-cyan-500/60 bg-cyan-500/10 text-slate-100'\n                    : 'border-slate-800 bg-[#0F141E] text-slate-300 hover:border-slate-700'\n                }`}\n              >\n                Target · {comparison.target.name} ({formatBodyName(comparison.target.body)})\n              </button>\n\n              {comparison.analogs.length === 0 ? (\n                <p className=\"border border-slate-800 bg-[#0F141E] px-2.5 py-2 text-xs text-slate-400\">\n                  No ranked Earth analogs available for {comparison.target.name}.\n                </p>\n              ) : (\n                comparison.analogs.map((analog) => (\n                  <button\n                    key={analog.location.id}\n                    type=\"button\"\n                    onClick={() => onSelectLocation(analog.location.id)}\n                    className={`w-full border px-2.5 py-1.5 text-left transition-colors focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-cyan-400 ${\n                      selected?.id === analog.location.id\n                        ? 'border-cyan-500/60 bg-cyan-500/10'\n                        : 'border-slate-800 bg-[#0F141E] hover:border-slate-700'\n                    }`}\n                  >\n                    <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between gap-2 text-xs\">\n                      <span className=\"font-semibold text-slate-100\">\n                        #{analog.rank} {analog.location.name}\n                      </span>\n                      <span className=\"font-mono font-semibold text-cyan-300 tabular-nums\">\n                        {analog.similarity_score.toFixed(1)}%\n                      </span>\n                    </div>\n                  </button>\n                ))\n              )}\n            </div>\n          </section>\n        )}\n\n        <AnalogPassport\n          comparison={comparison}\n          selectedAnalog={activeAnalogEntry}\n          profile={profile}\n          sources={dataset.sources}\n          loading={loading}\n          errorMessage={errorMessage}\n        />\n      </div>\n    </aside>\n  );\n}\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "src/components/Viewport3D.tsx",
      "bytes": 62259,
      "sha256": "6f92d348ebb5f743966b69a1bc1b884ca3ffdef0a9fa1cea4fe77836136fb05c",
      "content": "import React, { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';\nimport * as THREE from 'three';\nimport { OrbitControls } from 'three/addons/controls/OrbitControls.js';\nimport { PointerLockControls } from 'three/addons/controls/PointerLockControls.js';\nimport { RoomEnvironment } from 'three/addons/environments/RoomEnvironment.js';\nimport { Viewer, type SceneLoad } from '@mkkellogg/gaussian-splats-3d';\nimport { Compass, Eye, Footprints, Globe, Lock, Move, RotateCcw, TriangleAlert, Unlock } from 'lucide-react';\nimport type { Body, Location, LocationsDataset } from '@shared/types/analog-location';\nimport { resolveSameOriginAssetUrl } from '../data/loadDataset';\nimport { formatBodyName, formatCoordinateFrame, latLonToUnitSphere } from '../terrain/coordinates';\nimport {\n  readHeightfield,\n  sampleHeightAndSlope,\n  type HeightfieldData,\n} from '../terrain/heightfield';\nimport { canRenderSplatOnMetricTerrain } from '../terrain/splatUtils';\nimport { buildTerrainInWorker } from '../terrain/terrain-worker-client';\n\nexport type ViewportMode = 'orbit' | 'ground';\n\nexport type SplatStatus =\n  | { status: 'none'; message: string }\n  | { status: 'loading'; message: string }\n  | { status: 'ready'; gaussianCount: number; provenance: 'photogrammetric' | 'inferred' }\n  | { status: 'rejected'; message: string }\n  | { status: 'error'; message: string };\n\nexport interface ViewportTelemetry {\n  mode: ViewportMode;\n  fps: number | null;\n  frameTimeMs: number | null;\n  dpr: number;\n  isSoftwareGl: boolean;\n  localPosition_m: [number, number, number] | null;\n  absoluteElevation_m: number | null;\n  demSlopeDeg: number | null;\n  demTriangleCount: number | null;\n  verticalDatum: string | null;\n  verticalOrigin_m: number | null;\n  groundLoading: boolean;\n  groundError: string | null;\n  splatStatus: SplatStatus;\n  pointerLocked: boolean;\n  memoryUsedMiB: number | null;\n}\n\nexport interface Viewport3DProps {\n  selected: Location | null;\n  dataset: LocationsDataset;\n  onTargetSelected: (id: string) => void;\n  activeBody?: Body;\n  onBodyChange?: (body: Body) => void;\n  mode?: ViewportMode;\n  onModeChange?: (mode: ViewportMode) => void;\n  walkSpeedMps?: number;\n  eyeHeightM?: number;\n  onTelemetryUpdate?: (telemetry: ViewportTelemetry) => void;\n}\n\ninterface GlobeMarkerEntry {\n  location: Location;\n  role: 'target' | 'analog';\n  group: THREE.Group;\n  hitMesh: THREE.Mesh;\n  headMat: THREE.MeshStandardMaterial;\n  ringMat: THREE.MeshBasicMaterial;\n}\n\nconst MAX_DPR_BUDGET = 1.5;\nconst DPR_STEPS = [1.5, 1.25, 1.0, 0.85] as const;\n\n// Reusable scratch objects outside any RAF loop to prevent GC allocations.\nconst _raycaster = new THREE.Raycaster();\nconst _ndc = new THREE.Vector2();\nconst _upVec = new THREE.Vector3(0, 1, 0);\nconst _forwardVec = new THREE.Vector3();\nconst _rightVec = new THREE.Vector3();\nconst _moveVec = new THREE.Vector3();\nconst _projVec = new THREE.Vector3();\n\nfunction collectBodyMarkers(dataset: LocationsDataset, body: Body): Array<{ location: Location; role: 'target' | 'analog' }> {\n  const seen = new Set<string>();\n  const result: Array<{ location: Location; role: 'target' | 'analog' }> = [];\n\n  if (body === 'moon' || body === 'mars') {\n    for (const comp of dataset.comparisons) {\n      if (comp.target.body === body && !seen.has(comp.target.id)) {\n        seen.add(comp.target.id);\n        result.push({ location: comp.target, role: 'target' });\n      }\n    }\n    return result;\n  }\n\n  for (const comp of dataset.comparisons) {\n    for (const analog of comp.analogs) {\n      if (analog.location.body === 'earth' && !seen.has(analog.location.id)) {\n        seen.add(analog.location.id);\n        result.push({ location: analog.location, role: 'analog' });\n      }\n    }\n  }\n  return result;\n}\n\nfunction buildSchematicGraticule(radius: number): THREE.LineSegments {\n  const positions: number[] = [];\n  const colors: number[] = [];\n\n  const pushSegment = (\n    p1: [number, number, number],\n    p2: [number, number, number],\n    r: number,\n    g: number,\n    b: number,\n  ) => {\n    positions.push(p1[0], p1[1], p1[2], p2[0], p2[1], p2[2]);\n    colors.push(r, g, b, r, g, b);\n  };\n\n  // Parallels every 30 degrees (-60 to +60)\n  for (let lat = -60; lat <= 60; lat += 30) {\n    const isEquator = lat === 0;\n    const [cr, cg, cb] = isEquator ? [0.13, 0.72, 0.85] : [0.22, 0.3, 0.4];\n    for (let lon = -180; lon < 180; lon += 6) {\n      const p1 = latLonToUnitSphere(lat, lon, radius);\n      const p2 = latLonToUnitSphere(lat, lon + 6, radius);\n      pushSegment(p1, p2, cr, cg, cb);\n    }\n  }\n\n  // Meridians every 30 degrees (-180 to +150)\n  for (let lon = -180; lon < 180; lon += 30) {\n    const isPrime = lon === 0;\n    const isAntimeridian = lon === -180;\n    const [cr, cg, cb] = isPrime\n      ? [0.13, 0.72, 0.85]\n      : isAntimeridian\n        ? [0.65, 0.45, 0.16]\n        : [0.22, 0.3, 0.4];\n    for (let lat = -84; lat < 84; lat += 6) {\n      const p1 = latLonToUnitSphere(lat, lon, radius);\n      const p2 = latLonToUnitSphere(lat + 6, lon, radius);\n      pushSegment(p1, p2, cr, cg, cb);\n    }\n  }\n\n  const geo = new THREE.BufferGeometry();\n  geo.setAttribute('position', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(positions, 3));\n  geo.setAttribute('color', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(colors, 3));\n  const mat = new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({\n    vertexColors: true,\n    transparent: true,\n    opacity: 0.55,\n  });\n  return new THREE.LineSegments(geo, mat);\n}\n\nfunction disposeObject3DTree(root: THREE.Object3D): void {\n  root.traverse((obj) => {\n    const mesh = obj as THREE.Mesh;\n    if (mesh.geometry) {\n      mesh.geometry.dispose();\n    }\n    const mat = (mesh as { material?: THREE.Material | THREE.Material[] }).material;\n    if (Array.isArray(mat)) {\n      mat.forEach((m) => m.dispose());\n    } else if (mat) {\n      mat.dispose();\n    }\n  });\n}\n\nfunction readOptionalMemoryMiB(): number | null {\n  const perf = performance as unknown as {\n    memory?: { usedJSHeapSize?: number };\n  };\n  if (perf.memory && typeof perf.memory.usedJSHeapSize === 'number') {\n    return perf.memory.usedJSHeapSize / (1024 * 1024);\n  }\n  return null;\n}\n\nexport function Viewport3D({\n  selected,\n  dataset,\n  onTargetSelected,\n  activeBody: controlledBody,\n  onBodyChange,\n  mode: controlledMode,\n  onModeChange,\n  walkSpeedMps = 3.5,\n  eyeHeightM = 1.6,\n  onTelemetryUpdate,\n}: Viewport3DProps): React.JSX.Element {\n  const containerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);\n  const mountRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);\n\n  const [internalBody, setInternalBody] = useState<Body>(selected?.body ?? 'moon');\n  const [internalMode, setInternalMode] = useState<ViewportMode>('orbit');\n\n  const activeBody = controlledBody ?? internalBody;\n  const activeMode = controlledMode ?? internalMode;\n\n  const [webglError, setWebglError] = useState<string | null>(null);\n  const [contextLost, setContextLost] = useState(false);\n  const [rendererEpoch, setRendererEpoch] = useState(0);\n\n  const [pegmanDragging, setPegmanDragging] = useState(false);\n  const [pegmanScreenPos, setPegmanScreenPos] = useState<{ x: number; y: number } | null>(null);\n  const [pegmanHoverName, setPegmanHoverName] = useState<string | null>(null);\n  const [orbitNotice, setOrbitNotice] = useState<string | null>(null);\n\n  const [groundLoading, setGroundLoading] = useState(false);\n  const [groundError, setGroundError] = useState<string | null>(null);\n  const [splatStatus, setSplatStatus] = useState<SplatStatus>({\n    status: 'none',\n    message: '3D reconstruction not prepared',\n  });\n  const [pointerLocked, setPointerLocked] = useState(false);\n  const [groundHudReadout, setGroundHudReadout] = useState<{\n    fps: number | null;\n    frameTimeMs: number | null;\n    dpr: number;\n    pos: [number, number, number] | null;\n    absElev: number | null;\n    slopeDeg: number | null;\n  }>({\n    fps: null,\n    frameTimeMs: null,\n    dpr: 1,\n    pos: null,\n    absElev: null,\n    slopeDeg: null,\n  });\n\n  // Mutable refs accessed inside Three.js lifecycle without re-mounting renderer\n  const onTargetSelectedRef = useRef(onTargetSelected);\n  onTargetSelectedRef.current = onTargetSelected;\n  const onTelemetryUpdateRef = useRef(onTelemetryUpdate);\n  onTelemetryUpdateRef.current = onTelemetryUpdate;\n  const activeModeRef = useRef<ViewportMode>(activeMode);\n  activeModeRef.current = activeMode;\n  const walkSpeedRef = useRef(walkSpeedMps);\n  walkSpeedRef.current = walkSpeedMps;\n  const eyeHeightRef = useRef(eyeHeightM);\n  eyeHeightRef.current = eyeHeightM;\n\n  const setBody = (next: Body) => {\n    setOrbitNotice(null);\n    if (onBodyChange) onBodyChange(next);\n    else setInternalBody(next);\n  };\n\n  const setMode = (next: ViewportMode) => {\n    if (onModeChange) onModeChange(next);\n    else setInternalMode(next);\n  };\n\n  const canEnterGround = Boolean(\n    selected && selected.assets.heightmap !== null && selected.camera !== null,\n  );\n\n  const groundUnavailableReason = useMemo(() => {\n    if (!selected) return 'requires prepared DEM & camera metadata.';\n    if (selected.assets.heightmap === null) {\n      return `${selected.name} has no heightmap asset.`;\n    }\n    if (selected.camera === null) {\n      return `${selected.name} has no camera metadata.`;\n    }\n    return null;\n  }, [selected]);\n\n  // Auto-revert to orbit if selected location lacks ground assets while in ground mode.\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (activeMode === 'ground' && !canEnterGround) {\n      setMode('orbit');\n    }\n  }, [activeMode, canEnterGround]);\n\n  // Core Three.js refs\n  const rendererRef = useRef<THREE.WebGLRenderer | null>(null);\n  const sceneRef = useRef<THREE.Scene | null>(null);\n  const cameraRef = useRef<THREE.PerspectiveCamera | null>(null);\n  const orbitControlsRef = useRef<OrbitControls | null>(null);\n  const pointerLockControlsRef = useRef<PointerLockControls | null>(null);\n  const orbitGroupRef = useRef<THREE.Group | null>(null);\n  const groundGroupRef = useRef<THREE.Group | null>(null);\n  const globeMeshRef = useRef<THREE.Mesh | null>(null);\n  const markerEntriesRef = useRef<GlobeMarkerEntry[]>([]);\n  const isSoftwareGlRef = useRef(false);\n  const dprStepIdxRef = useRef(0);\n  const requestOrbitRenderRef = useRef<() => void>(() => {});\n\n  // Ground scene runtime state refs\n  const groundGenerationRef = useRef(0);\n  const activeHeightfieldRef = useRef<HeightfieldData | null>(null);\n  const activeSplatViewerRef = useRef<Viewer | null>(null);\n  const splatReadyRef = useRef(false);\n  const splatQueueRef = useRef<Promise<void>>(Promise.resolve());\n  const disposalChainRef = useRef<Promise<void>>(Promise.resolve());\n  const pressedKeysRef = useRef<Set<string>>(new Set());\n  const userMovedOnGroundRef = useRef(false);\n  const demTriangleCountRef = useRef<number | null>(null);\n  const verticalDatumRef = useRef<string | null>(null);\n  const verticalOriginRef = useRef<number | null>(null);\n  const latestTelemetryStateRef = useRef<{\n    groundLoading: boolean;\n    groundError: string | null;\n    splatStatus: SplatStatus;\n    pointerLocked: boolean;\n  }>({\n    groundLoading: false,\n    groundError: null,\n    splatStatus: { status: 'none', message: '3D reconstruction not prepared' },\n    pointerLocked: false,\n  });\n\n  latestTelemetryStateRef.current = {\n    groundLoading,\n    groundError,\n    splatStatus,\n    pointerLocked,\n  };\n\n  // Emit telemetry when discrete status changes in Orbit/Ground\n  useEffect(() => {\n    onTelemetryUpdateRef.current?.({\n      mode: activeMode,\n      fps: groundHudReadout.fps,\n      frameTimeMs: groundHudReadout.frameTimeMs,\n      dpr: groundHudReadout.dpr,\n      isSoftwareGl: isSoftwareGlRef.current,\n      localPosition_m: groundHudReadout.pos,\n      absoluteElevation_m: groundHudReadout.absElev,\n      demSlopeDeg: groundHudReadout.slopeDeg,\n      demTriangleCount: demTriangleCountRef.current,\n      verticalDatum: verticalDatumRef.current,\n      verticalOrigin_m: verticalOriginRef.current,\n      groundLoading,\n      groundError,\n      splatStatus,\n      pointerLocked,\n      memoryUsedMiB: readOptionalMemoryMiB(),\n    });\n  }, [\n    activeMode,\n    groundHudReadout,\n    groundLoading,\n    groundError,\n    splatStatus,\n    pointerLocked,\n  ]);\n\n  // 1. Mount WebGLRenderer once per rendererEpoch, StrictMode-safe with serialized cleanup\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const mountEl = mountRef.current;\n    if (!mountEl) return;\n\n    let cancelled = false;\n    let frameId = 0;\n    let timeoutId = 0;\n    let resizeObserver: ResizeObserver | null = null;\n\n    // Probe WebGL availability & software-GL status before constructing Three renderer\n    const probeCanvas = document.createElement('canvas');\n    const probeGl =\n      (probeCanvas.getContext('webgl2') as WebGL2RenderingContext | null) ??\n      (probeCanvas.getContext('webgl') as WebGLRenderingContext | null);\n    if (!probeGl) {\n      setWebglError('WebGL is unavailable in this browser/device. 3D viewport cannot render.');\n      return;\n    }\n\n    const debugExt = probeGl.getExtension('WEBGL_debug_renderer_info');\n    const rendererStr = String(\n      probeGl.getParameter(debugExt ? debugExt.UNMASKED_RENDERER_WEBGL : probeGl.RENDERER) ?? '',\n    );\n    const isSoftwareGl = /SwiftShader|llvmpipe|softpipe|Software|Basic Render/i.test(rendererStr);\n    probeGl.getExtension('WEBGL_lose_context')?.loseContext();\n    isSoftwareGlRef.current = isSoftwareGl;\n    setWebglError(null);\n    setContextLost(false);\n\n    const initAfterPendingDispose = async () => {\n      await disposalChainRef.current.catch(() => {});\n      if (cancelled) return;\n\n      // Guarantee single canvas child\n      while (mountEl.firstChild) {\n        mountEl.removeChild(mountEl.firstChild);\n      }\n\n      const width = Math.max(mountEl.clientWidth, 1);\n      const height = Math.max(mountEl.clientHeight, 1);\n\n      const renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({\n        antialias: !isSoftwareGl,\n        powerPreference: 'high-performance',\n      });\n      renderer.outputColorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace;\n      renderer.toneMapping = THREE.ACESFilmicToneMapping;\n      renderer.toneMappingExposure = 1.08;\n      const initialDpr = isSoftwareGl ? 1 : Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, MAX_DPR_BUDGET);\n      dprStepIdxRef.current = DPR_STEPS.findIndex((s) => s <= initialDpr);\n      if (dprStepIdxRef.current < 0) dprStepIdxRef.current = DPR_STEPS.length - 1;\n      renderer.setPixelRatio(initialDpr);\n      renderer.setSize(width, height, false);\n      renderer.domElement.style.width = '100%';\n      renderer.domElement.style.height = '100%';\n      renderer.domElement.style.display = 'block';\n      renderer.domElement.setAttribute('data-testid', 'viewport3d-canvas');\n      mountEl.appendChild(renderer.domElement);\n\n      const scene = new THREE.Scene();\n      scene.background = new THREE.Color(0x07090e);\n\n      const pmrem = new THREE.PMREMGenerator(renderer);\n      const envTexture = pmrem.fromScene(new RoomEnvironment(), 0.04).texture;\n      scene.environment = envTexture;\n      pmrem.dispose();\n\n      const hemiLight = new THREE.HemisphereLight(0xbcd4f5, 0x1a1e26, 0.85);\n      scene.add(hemiLight);\n      const keyLight = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xfff1dc, 1.55);\n      keyLight.position.set(8, 12, 6);\n      scene.add(keyLight);\n      const rimLight = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0x52a8d8, 0.45);\n      rimLight.position.set(-8, -4, -6);\n      scene.add(rimLight);\n\n      const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(42, width / height, 0.05, 5000);\n      camera.position.set(0, 0.72, 3.35);\n      camera.lookAt(0, 0, 0);\n\n      const orbitGroup = new THREE.Group();\n      orbitGroup.name = 'orbit-schematic-group';\n      scene.add(orbitGroup);\n\n      const globeGeometry = new THREE.SphereGeometry(1, 48, 36);\n      const globeMaterial = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({\n        color: 0x242b36,\n        roughness: 0.9,\n        metalness: 0.06,\n      });\n      const globeMesh = new THREE.Mesh(globeGeometry, globeMaterial);\n      globeMesh.name = 'schematic-reference-globe';\n      orbitGroup.add(globeMesh);\n\n      const graticule = buildSchematicGraticule(1.003);\n      orbitGroup.add(graticule);\n\n      const groundGroup = new THREE.Group();\n      groundGroup.name = 'ground-terrain-group';\n      groundGroup.visible = false;\n      scene.add(groundGroup);\n\n      const orbitControls = new OrbitControls(camera, renderer.domElement);\n      orbitControls.enablePan = false;\n      orbitControls.minDistance = 1.45;\n      orbitControls.maxDistance = 6.0;\n      orbitControls.enableDamping = !isSoftwareGl;\n      orbitControls.dampingFactor = 0.07;\n\n      const pointerLockControls = new PointerLockControls(camera, renderer.domElement);\n\n      rendererRef.current = renderer;\n      sceneRef.current = scene;\n      cameraRef.current = camera;\n      orbitControlsRef.current = orbitControls;\n      pointerLockControlsRef.current = pointerLockControls;\n      orbitGroupRef.current = orbitGroup;\n      groundGroupRef.current = groundGroup;\n      globeMeshRef.current = globeMesh;\n\n      if (!isSoftwareGl) {\n        renderer.compile(scene, camera);\n      }\n\n      // Context loss / restore handlers\n      let isContextLostLocal = false;\n      const handleContextLost = (e: Event) => {\n        e.preventDefault();\n        isContextLostLocal = true;\n        pressedKeysRef.current.clear();\n        window.cancelAnimationFrame(frameId);\n        window.clearTimeout(timeoutId);\n        setContextLost(true);\n      };\n      const handleContextRestored = () => {\n        isContextLostLocal = false;\n        setContextLost(false);\n        requestOrbitRender();\n      };\n      renderer.domElement.addEventListener('webglcontextlost', handleContextLost);\n      renderer.domElement.addEventListener('webglcontextrestored', handleContextRestored);\n\n      // PointerLock lifecycle + key hygiene\n      const clearKeys = () => {\n        pressedKeysRef.current.clear();\n      };\n      const handleLock = () => {\n        setPointerLocked(true);\n      };\n      const handleUnlock = () => {\n        clearKeys();\n        setPointerLocked(false);\n      };\n      pointerLockControls.addEventListener('lock', handleLock);\n      pointerLockControls.addEventListener('unlock', handleUnlock);\n\n      const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {\n        if (activeModeRef.current !== 'ground') return;\n        const tag = (e.target as HTMLElement | null)?.tagName;\n        if (tag === 'INPUT' || tag === 'SELECT' || tag === 'TEXTAREA') return;\n        if (\n          e.code === 'KeyW' ||\n          e.code === 'KeyA' ||\n          e.code === 'KeyS' ||\n          e.code === 'KeyD' ||\n          e.code === 'ArrowUp' ||\n          e.code === 'ArrowDown' ||\n          e.code === 'ArrowLeft' ||\n          e.code === 'ArrowRight'\n        ) {\n          pressedKeysRef.current.add(e.code);\n        }\n      };\n      const handleKeyUp = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {\n        pressedKeysRef.current.delete(e.code);\n      };\n      const handleVisibilityChange = () => {\n        if (document.hidden) clearKeys();\n      };\n\n      window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);\n      window.addEventListener('keyup', handleKeyUp);\n      window.addEventListener('blur', clearKeys);\n      document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', handleVisibilityChange);\n\n      // Software-WebGL fence pacing helper (threejs skill)\n      const afterDraw = (cb: () => void) => {\n        if (cancelled || isContextLostLocal) return;\n        const gl = renderer.getContext();\n        if ('fenceSync' in gl) {\n          const gl2 = gl as WebGL2RenderingContext;\n          const sync = gl2.fenceSync(gl2.SYNC_GPU_COMMANDS_COMPLETE, 0);\n          if (sync) {\n            gl2.flush();\n            const poll = () => {\n              if (cancelled || isContextLostLocal) {\n                gl2.deleteSync(sync);\n                return;\n              }\n              const status = gl2.getSyncParameter(sync, gl2.SYNC_STATUS);\n              if (status === gl2.UNSIGNALED) {\n                timeoutId = window.setTimeout(poll, 5);\n              } else {\n                gl2.deleteSync(sync);\n                timeoutId = window.setTimeout(cb, 80);\n              }\n            };\n            timeoutId = window.setTimeout(poll, 5);\n            return;\n          }\n        }\n        timeoutId = window.setTimeout(cb, 80);\n      };\n\n      // Coalesced on-demand renderer for Orbit mode\n      let orbitRenderBusy = false;\n      let orbitRenderDirty = false;\n      const drawCurrentFrame = () => {\n        if (cancelled || isContextLostLocal) return;\n        const size = new THREE.Vector2();\n        renderer.getSize(size);\n        if (size.x <= 0 || size.y <= 0) return;\n\n        if (activeModeRef.current === 'ground' && splatReadyRef.current && activeSplatViewerRef.current) {\n          activeSplatViewerRef.current.update();\n          activeSplatViewerRef.current.render();\n        } else {\n          renderer.render(scene, camera);\n        }\n      };\n\n      const requestOrbitRender = () => {\n        if (cancelled || isContextLostLocal || activeModeRef.current !== 'orbit') return;\n        if (orbitRenderBusy) {\n          orbitRenderDirty = true;\n          return;\n        }\n        orbitRenderBusy = true;\n        frameId = window.requestAnimationFrame(() => {\n          orbitRenderDirty = false;\n          if (cancelled || isContextLostLocal) {\n            orbitRenderBusy = false;\n            return;\n          }\n          const moved = orbitControls.update();\n          drawCurrentFrame();\n          const done = () => {\n            orbitRenderBusy = false;\n            if (orbitRenderDirty || (moved && orbitControls.enableDamping && activeModeRef.current === 'orbit')) {\n              requestOrbitRender();\n            }\n          };\n          if (isSoftwareGl) afterDraw(done);\n          else done();\n        });\n      };\n\n      requestOrbitRenderRef.current = requestOrbitRender;\n      orbitControls.addEventListener('change', requestOrbitRender);\n\n      // Ground loop with real FPS/frame-time measurement, adaptive DPR with hysteresis, and 4Hz HUD throttle\n      let lastFrameTs = performance.now();\n      let lastHudEmitTs = 0;\n      let frameWindowCount = 0;\n      let frameWindowSumMs = 0;\n      let windowStartTs = performance.now();\n      let slowWindows = 0;\n      let fastWindows = 0;\n\n      const stepGroundMovement = (dtSec: number) => {\n        const grid = activeHeightfieldRef.current;\n        if (!grid || activeModeRef.current !== 'ground') return;\n\n        const keys = pressedKeysRef.current;\n        const forward = (keys.has('KeyW') || keys.has('ArrowUp') ? 1 : 0) -\n          (keys.has('KeyS') || keys.has('ArrowDown') ? 1 : 0);\n        const strafe = (keys.has('KeyD') || keys.has('ArrowRight') ? 1 : 0) -\n          (keys.has('KeyA') || keys.has('ArrowLeft') ? 1 : 0);\n\n        if (forward === 0 && strafe === 0) return;\n\n        userMovedOnGroundRef.current = true;\n        camera.getWorldDirection(_forwardVec);\n        _forwardVec.y = 0;\n        if (_forwardVec.lengthSq() < 1e-8) {\n          _forwardVec.set(0, 0, -1);\n        } else {\n          _forwardVec.normalize();\n        }\n        _rightVec.crossVectors(_forwardVec, _upVec).normalize();\n\n        _moveVec\n          .set(0, 0, 0)\n          .addScaledVector(_forwardVec, forward)\n          .addScaledVector(_rightVec, strafe);\n        if (_moveVec.lengthSq() > 0) {\n          _moveVec.normalize();\n        }\n\n        const stepDist = walkSpeedRef.current * dtSec;\n        const curX = camera.position.x;\n        const curZ = camera.position.z;\n        const targetX = curX + _moveVec.x * stepDist;\n        const targetZ = curZ + _moveVec.z * stepDist;\n\n        // Validate against exact DEM triangles; fall back to axis-sliding if diagonal hits boundary/NoData\n        const fullSample = sampleHeightAndSlope(grid, targetX, targetZ);\n        if (fullSample !== null) {\n          camera.position.set(targetX, fullSample.y_m + eyeHeightRef.current, targetZ);\n          return;\n        }\n        const xOnly = sampleHeightAndSlope(grid, targetX, curZ);\n        if (xOnly !== null) {\n          camera.position.set(targetX, xOnly.y_m + eyeHeightRef.current, curZ);\n          return;\n        }\n        const zOnly = sampleHeightAndSlope(grid, curX, targetZ);\n        if (zOnly !== null) {\n          camera.position.set(curX, zOnly.y_m + eyeHeightRef.current, targetZ);\n        }\n      };\n\n      const updateAdaptiveDpr = (avgMs: number) => {\n        if (isSoftwareGl) return;\n        const maxDpr = Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, MAX_DPR_BUDGET);\n        if (avgMs > 24) {\n          slowWindows += 1;\n          fastWindows = 0;\n          if (slowWindows >= 2 && dprStepIdxRef.current < DPR_STEPS.length - 1) {\n            dprStepIdxRef.current += 1;\n            const nextDpr = Math.min(DPR_STEPS[dprStepIdxRef.current], maxDpr);\n            renderer.setPixelRatio(nextDpr);\n            slowWindows = 0;\n          }\n        } else if (avgMs < 12) {\n
