      "sha256": "ca0881e2f9b5b2bc06bafac3709e7ac4d6a0b48bf4aab1cabc562ca01673d54c",
      "content": "import * as THREE from 'three';\nimport type { Mat4, Splat } from '@shared/types/analog-location';\n\nexport const MAX_SPLATS_BUDGET = 250_000;\nexport const MAX_SCENE_ASSET_BYTES_BUDGET = 64 * 1024 * 1024; // 64 MiB\n\nexport interface DecomposedSplatTransform {\n  position: [number, number, number];\n  quaternion: [number, number, number, number];\n  scale: [number, number, number];\n}\n\nexport type SplatValidationResult =\n  | { ok: true; transform: DecomposedSplatTransform }\n  | { ok: false; reason: string };\n\n/**\n * Validates a column-major 4x4 affine matrix (Three.js Matrix4 order) and decomposes it into\n * position, quaternion (XYZW), and positive scale. Rejects non-finite entries, singular matrices,\n * reflections (negative scale/determinant), projective bottom row, and unsupported shear.\n */\nexport function validateAndDecomposeSplatMatrix(worldFromAsset: Mat4): SplatValidationResult {\n  if (worldFromAsset.length !== 16) {\n    return { ok: false, reason: 'world_from_asset must contain 16 elements.' };\n  }\n  for (let i = 0; i < 16; i += 1) {\n    if (!Number.isFinite(worldFromAsset[i])) {\n      return { ok: false, reason: `world_from_asset[${i}] is not finite.` };\n    }\n  }\n\n  // Column-major affine check: elements [3], [7], [11] are bottom row x/y/z, [15] is bottom-right w.\n  if (\n    Math.abs(worldFromAsset[3]) > 1e-6 ||\n    Math.abs(worldFromAsset[7]) > 1e-6 ||\n    Math.abs(worldFromAsset[11]) > 1e-6 ||\n    Math.abs(worldFromAsset[15] - 1) > 1e-6\n  ) {\n    return {\n      ok: false,\n      reason: 'world_from_asset must be an affine transform with bottom row [0, 0, 0, 1].',\n    };\n  }\n\n  const matrix = new THREE.Matrix4().fromArray(worldFromAsset);\n  const det = matrix.determinant();\n  if (!Number.isFinite(det) || Math.abs(det) <= 1e-9) {\n    return { ok: false, reason: 'world_from_asset is singular (determinant near zero).' };\n  }\n  if (det < 0) {\n    return {\n      ok: false,\n      reason: 'world_from_asset has negative determinant (reflection is unsupported).',\n    };\n  }\n\n  const position = new THREE.Vector3();\n  const quaternion = new THREE.Quaternion();\n  const scale = new THREE.Vector3();\n  matrix.decompose(position, quaternion, scale);\n\n  if (\n    !Number.isFinite(position.x) ||\n    !Number.isFinite(position.y) ||\n    !Number.isFinite(position.z) ||\n    !Number.isFinite(quaternion.x) ||\n    !Number.isFinite(quaternion.y) ||\n    !Number.isFinite(quaternion.z) ||\n    !Number.isFinite(quaternion.w) ||\n    !Number.isFinite(scale.x) ||\n    !Number.isFinite(scale.y) ||\n    !Number.isFinite(scale.z)\n  ) {\n    return { ok: false, reason: 'Decomposed splat transform produced non-finite values.' };\n  }\n\n  if (scale.x <= 1e-9 || scale.y <= 1e-9 || scale.z <= 1e-9) {\n    return {\n      ok: false,\n      reason: 'world_from_asset must have strictly positive scale components.',\n    };\n  }\n\n  const recomposed = new THREE.Matrix4().compose(position, quaternion, scale);\n  let maxResidual = 0;\n  for (let i = 0; i < 16; i += 1) {\n    const diff = Math.abs(recomposed.elements[i] - worldFromAsset[i]);\n    if (diff > maxResidual) maxResidual = diff;\n  }\n  const scaleNorm = Math.max(1, scale.x, scale.y, scale.z);\n  if (maxResidual > 1e-3 * scaleNorm) {\n    return {\n      ok: false,\n      reason: 'world_from_asset contains unsupported shear or skew; cannot decompose losslessly.',\n    };\n  }\n\n  return {\n    ok: true,\n    transform: {\n      position: [position.x, position.y, position.z],\n      quaternion: [quaternion.x, quaternion.y, quaternion.z, quaternion.w],\n      scale: [scale.x, scale.y, scale.z],\n    },\n  };\n}\n\n/**\n * Checks whether a splat asset is scientifically eligible and within engineering budgets\n * to be overlaid on top of a metric DEM heightfield.\n */\nexport function canRenderSplatOnMetricTerrain(\n  splat: Splat,\n  otherSceneAssetBytes = 0,\n): SplatValidationResult {\n  if (splat.format !== 'splat' && splat.format !== 'ply') {\n    return { ok: false, reason: `Unsupported splat format: ${String(splat.format)}.` };\n  }\n  if (!splat.scale_is_metric) {\n    return {\n      ok: false,\n      reason: 'Splat reconstruction has scale_is_metric=false; cannot overlay on metric terrain.',\n    };\n  }\n  if (splat.geometry_provenance !== 'photogrammetric') {\n    return {\n      ok: false,\n      reason: `Splat geometry provenance is \"${splat.geometry_provenance}\" (model-derived visualization only, not metric terrain).`,\n    };\n  }\n  if (splat.gaussian_count > MAX_SPLATS_BUDGET) {\n    return {\n      ok: false,\n      reason: `Splat count (${splat.gaussian_count.toLocaleString()}) exceeds engineering budget (${MAX_SPLATS_BUDGET.toLocaleString()}).`,\n    };\n  }\n  const totalBytes = splat.asset.bytes + otherSceneAssetBytes;\n  if (totalBytes > MAX_SCENE_ASSET_BYTES_BUDGET) {\n    return {\n      ok: false,\n      reason: `Total ground scene asset size (${(totalBytes / (1024 * 1024)).toFixed(1)} MiB) exceeds 64 MiB budget.`,\n    };\n  }\n\n  return validateAndDecomposeSplatMatrix(splat.world_from_asset);\n}\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "src/terrain/terrain-worker-client.ts",
      "bytes": 2426,
      "sha256": "4a134ba13f0b6fb0167fb3cc3cedb9b5c0e9164d56db63c2b529d281aa6df0b4",
      "content": "import type { HeightfieldData } from './heightfield';\n\nexport interface TerrainBuffers {\n  positions: Float32Array;\n  normals: Float32Array;\n  indices: Uint32Array;\n}\n\n/**\n * Builds terrain BufferGeometry arrays in a dedicated module WebWorker.\n * Copies height/valid buffers before transfer so the caller's collision sampler buffers\n * are never detached. Always terminates the worker on completion, error, or abort.\n */\nexport function buildTerrainInWorker(\n  grid: HeightfieldData,\n  signal: AbortSignal,\n): Promise<TerrainBuffers> {\n  if (signal.aborted) {\n    return Promise.reject(new DOMException('Terrain build aborted.', 'AbortError'));\n  }\n\n  return new Promise<TerrainBuffers>((resolve, reject) => {\n    const worker = new Worker(new URL('./terrain.worker.ts', import.meta.url), {\n      type: 'module',\n    });\n\n    let settled = false;\n\n    const cleanup = () => {\n      signal.removeEventListener('abort', onAbort);\n      worker.onmessage = null;\n      worker.onerror = null;\n      worker.terminate();\n    };\n\n    const onAbort = () => {\n      if (settled) return;\n      settled = true;\n      cleanup();\n      reject(new DOMException('Terrain build aborted.', 'AbortError'));\n    };\n\n    signal.addEventListener('abort', onAbort, { once: true });\n\n    worker.onmessage = (\n      event: MessageEvent<\n        | { type: 'ok'; positions: Float32Array; normals: Float32Array; indices: Uint32Array }\n        | { type: 'error'; message: string }\n      >,\n    ) => {\n      if (settled) return;\n      settled = true;\n      cleanup();\n      if (event.data.type === 'ok') {\n        resolve({\n          positions: event.data.positions,\n          normals: event.data.normals,\n          indices: event.data.indices,\n        });\n      } else {\n        reject(new Error(event.data.message));\n      }\n    };\n\n    worker.onerror = (event) => {\n      if (settled) return;\n      settled = true;\n      cleanup();\n      reject(new Error(event.message || 'Terrain worker execution failed.'));\n    };\n\n    // Clone buffers so the main-thread collision sampler retains ownership without detachment.\n    const heightsCopy = new Float32Array(grid.heights);\n    const validCopy = new Uint8Array(grid.valid);\n\n    worker.postMessage(\n      {\n        type: 'build',\n        grid: {\n          ...grid,\n          heights: heightsCopy,\n          valid: validCopy,\n        },\n      },\n      [heightsCopy.buffer, validCopy.buffer],\n    );\n  });\n}\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "src/terrain/terrain.worker.ts",
      "bytes": 1083,
      "sha256": "99ff902aad2137861a47d48916b4e55e726b68fed18a5ba2f4312b4f98a68a6b",
      "content": "import { buildTerrainGeometryData, type HeightfieldData } from './heightfield';\n\ninterface WorkerRequest {\n  type: 'build';\n  grid: HeightfieldData;\n}\n\ninterface WorkerSuccessResponse {\n  type: 'ok';\n  positions: Float32Array;\n  normals: Float32Array;\n  indices: Uint32Array;\n}\n\ninterface WorkerErrorResponse {\n  type: 'error';\n  message: string;\n}\n\ninterface WorkerScope {\n  onmessage: ((event: MessageEvent<WorkerRequest>) => void) | null;\n  postMessage(message: WorkerSuccessResponse | WorkerErrorResponse, transfer?: Transferable[]): void;\n}\n\nconst workerScope = globalThis as unknown as WorkerScope;\n\nworkerScope.onmessage = (event: MessageEvent<WorkerRequest>) => {\n  try {\n    const { grid } = event.data;\n    const { positions, normals, indices } = buildTerrainGeometryData(grid);\n    workerScope.postMessage(\n      { type: 'ok', positions, normals, indices },\n      [positions.buffer, normals.buffer, indices.buffer],\n    );\n  } catch (err) {\n    workerScope.postMessage({\n      type: 'error',\n      message: err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err),\n    });\n  }\n};\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "src/types/gaussian-splats-3d.d.ts",
      "bytes": 932,
      "sha256": "f7f260dc482372bdcb4b57e07a3a3272a245e349ee8d6c5cd83fd33c5da7d015",
      "content": "declare module '@mkkellogg/gaussian-splats-3d' {\n  import type { Camera, Scene, WebGLRenderer } from 'three';\n  export interface SceneLoad {\n    readonly promise: Promise<void>;\n    abort(reason?: string): void;\n  }\n  export class Viewer {\n    constructor(options: {\n      renderer: WebGLRenderer;\n      camera: Camera;\n      threeScene: Scene;\n      rootElement: HTMLElement;\n      selfDrivenMode: false;\n      useBuiltInControls: false;\n      sharedMemoryForWorkers: false;\n      gpuAcceleratedSort?: boolean;\n      integerBasedSort?: boolean;\n      sphericalHarmonicsDegree?: number;\n    });\n    addSplatScene(path: string, options?: {\n      showLoadingUI?: boolean;\n      progressiveLoad?: boolean;\n      position?: [number, number, number];\n      rotation?: [number, number, number, number];\n      scale?: [number, number, number];\n    }): SceneLoad;\n    update(): void;\n    render(): void;\n    dispose(): Promise<void>;\n  }\n}\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "src/vite-env.d.ts",
      "bytes": 38,
      "sha256": "65996936fbb042915f7b74a200fcdde7e410f32a669b1ab9597cfaa4b0faddb5",
      "content": "/// <reference types=\"vite/client\" />\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "tests/agent-harness.html",
      "bytes": 383,
      "sha256": "93c7ad19989113b0cb6b72d976172921260eec8f95e99435ce7ccc5fc36d30c7",
      "content": "<!doctype html>\n<html lang=\"en\">\n  <head>\n    <meta charset=\"UTF-8\" />\n    <meta name=\"viewport\" content=\"width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0\" />\n    <title>UI SDK Agent Test Harness (TEST_ONLY)</title>\n  </head>\n  <body class=\"bg-[#07090E] text-slate-100 p-4\">\n    <div id=\"harness-root\"></div>\n    <script type=\"module\" src=\"/tests/agent-harness.tsx\"></script>\n  </body>\n</html>\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "tests/agent-harness.tsx",
      "bytes": 7107,
      "sha256": "7effbac3051aafe84511934fa9462410f65394dba1414977f1e58300e1e6926c",
      "content": "import React, { StrictMode, useState } from 'react';\nimport { createRoot } from 'react-dom/client';\nimport { createAgent } from '@ui-sdk/react/agent';\nimport type { Comparison, ScoringProfile, Source } from '@shared/types/analog-location';\nimport { AnalogPassport } from '../src/components/AnalogPassport';\nimport '../src/styles.css';\n\n// Created BEFORE first React render so @ui-sdk/react/agent observes every disclosure primitive.\nconst agent = createAgent();\n\ndeclare global {\n  interface Window {\n    __TEST_ONLY_AGENT__?: ReturnType<typeof createAgent>;\n  }\n}\n\nwindow.__TEST_ONLY_AGENT__ = agent;\n\n// Strictly TEST_ONLY fixture used solely inside the agent/mismatch harness (never shipped in dist or public/data).\nconst TEST_ONLY_SOURCES: Source[] = [\n  {\n    id: 'src_test_only_1',\n    provider: 'TEST_ONLY Provider',\n    product_id: 'TEST_PROD_01',\n    product_url: 'https://example.org/test-only-product',\n    mission: 'TEST_MISSION',\n    access_url: 'https://example.org/archive',\n    retrieved_at: '2026-01-01T00:00:00Z',\n    sha256: 'a'.repeat(64),\n    license: 'CC0-1.0',\n    local_path: 'raw/test_only_dem.tif',\n  },\n];\n\nconst TEST_ONLY_MISMATCH_PROFILE: ScoringProfile = {\n  id: 'profile_mismatch_test_only',\n  version: '1.0.0',\n  algorithm: 'weighted_cosine_euclidean_v1',\n  alpha: 0.5,\n  features: [\n    {\n      key: 'slope_mean_deg',\n      unit: 'deg',\n      quantity: 'mean terrain slope',\n      weight: 1,\n      lower: 0,\n      upper: 45,\n      calibration_source_ids: ['src_test_only_1'],\n      calibration_method: 'TEST_ONLY_CALIBRATION',\n    },\n  ],\n  comparison_resolution_m: 10,\n  window_m: 100,\n  min_valid_area_fraction: 0.8,\n};\n\nfunction makeEmptyFeature<U extends 'deg' | '1' | 'm' | 'degC' | 'kWh/m2/day'>(\n  unit: U,\n  quantity: string,\n) {\n  return {\n    unit,\n    quantity,\n    method: 'TEST_ONLY',\n    spatial_resolution_m: null,\n    support_window_m: null,\n    period: null,\n    value: null,\n    source_ids: [],\n    missing_reason: 'TEST_ONLY missing observation',\n  };\n}\n\nconst TEST_ONLY_COMPARISON: Comparison = {\n  profile_id: 'profile_expected_test_only',\n  target: {\n    id: 'test_only_target',\n    name: 'TEST_ONLY Lunar Target',\n    body: 'moon',\n    coordinates: {\n      latitude_deg: 0,\n      longitude_deg: 0,\n      frame: 'moon_iau_planetocentric',\n      source_ids: ['src_test_only_1'],\n    },\n    aoi: { local_path: 'aoi/test_only.geojson', source_ids: ['src_test_only_1'] },\n    missions: ['TEST_ONLY'],\n    features: {\n      slope_mean_deg: makeEmptyFeature('deg', 'mean slope'),\n      slope_max_deg: makeEmptyFeature('deg', 'max slope'),\n      roughness_index: makeEmptyFeature('1', 'roughness'),\n      elevation_delta_m: makeEmptyFeature('m', 'elevation delta'),\n      temp_min_c: makeEmptyFeature('degC', 'min temperature'),\n      temp_max_c: makeEmptyFeature('degC', 'max temperature'),\n      solar_irradiance_kwh_m2: makeEmptyFeature('kWh/m2/day', 'daily solar irradiation'),\n      aridity_index: makeEmptyFeature('1', 'aridity'),\n      albedo: makeEmptyFeature('1', 'albedo'),\n    },\n    geology: {\n      classes: [],\n      method: 'TEST_ONLY',\n      source_ids: [],\n      missing_reason: 'TEST_ONLY geology omitted',\n    },\n    assets: { dem: null, heightmap: null, splat: null },\n    camera: null,\n  },\n  analogs: [\n    {\n      rank: 1,\n      similarity_score: 50,\n      coverage: 0.5,\n      location: {\n        id: 'test_only_earth',\n        name: 'TEST_ONLY Earth Site',\n        body: 'earth',\n        coordinates: {\n          latitude_deg: 0,\n          longitude_deg: 0,\n          frame: 'earth_wgs84',\n          source_ids: ['src_test_only_1'],\n        },\n        aoi: { local_path: 'aoi/test_only_earth.geojson', source_ids: ['src_test_only_1'] },\n        missions: [],\n        features: {\n          slope_mean_deg: makeEmptyFeature('deg', 'mean slope'),\n          slope_max_deg: makeEmptyFeature('deg', 'max slope'),\n          roughness_index: makeEmptyFeature('1', 'roughness'),\n          elevation_delta_m: makeEmptyFeature('m', 'elevation delta'),\n          temp_min_c: makeEmptyFeature('degC', 'min temperature'),\n          temp_max_c: makeEmptyFeature('degC', 'max temperature'),\n          solar_irradiance_kwh_m2: makeEmptyFeature('kWh/m2/day', 'daily solar irradiation'),\n          aridity_index: makeEmptyFeature('1', 'aridity'),\n          albedo: makeEmptyFeature('1', 'albedo'),\n        },\n        geology: {\n          classes: [],\n          method: 'TEST_ONLY',\n          source_ids: [],\n          missing_reason: 'TEST_ONLY geology omitted',\n        },\n        assets: { dem: null, heightmap: null, splat: null },\n        camera: null,\n      },\n      breakdown: {\n        slope_mean_deg: { similarity_pct: null, squared_distance_contribution: null, reason: 'TEST_ONLY' },\n        slope_max_deg: { similarity_pct: null, squared_distance_contribution: null, reason: 'TEST_ONLY' },\n        roughness_index: { similarity_pct: null, squared_distance_contribution: null, reason: 'TEST_ONLY' },\n        elevation_delta_m: { similarity_pct: null, squared_distance_contribution: null, reason: 'TEST_ONLY' },\n        temp_min_c: { similarity_pct: null, squared_distance_contribution: null, reason: 'TEST_ONLY' },\n        temp_max_c: { similarity_pct: null, squared_distance_contribution: null, reason: 'TEST_ONLY' },\n        solar_irradiance_kwh_m2: { similarity_pct: null, squared_distance_contribution: null, reason: 'TEST_ONLY' },\n        aridity_index: { similarity_pct: null, squared_distance_contribution: null, reason: 'TEST_ONLY' },\n        albedo: { similarity_pct: null, squared_distance_contribution: null, reason: 'TEST_ONLY' },\n      },\n    },\n  ],\n  excluded: [],\n};\n\nfunction HarnessApp(): React.JSX.Element {\n  const [mounted, setMounted] = useState(true);\n  const [mismatchMode, setMismatchMode] = useState(false);\n\n  return (\n    <main className=\"mx-auto max-w-md space-y-3\">\n      <div className=\"flex gap-2\">\n        <button\n          type=\"button\"\n          data-testid=\"harness-toggle-mount\"\n          onClick={() => setMounted((v) => !v)}\n          className=\"border border-slate-700 bg-slate-900 px-2.5 py-1 text-xs\"\n        >\n          {mounted ? 'Unmount Passport' : 'Mount Passport'}\n        </button>\n        <button\n          type=\"button\"\n          data-testid=\"harness-toggle-mismatch\"\n          onClick={() => setMismatchMode((v) => !v)}\n          className=\"border border-slate-700 bg-slate-900 px-2.5 py-1 text-xs\"\n        >\n          {mismatchMode ? 'Use Empty' : 'Use TEST_ONLY Profile Mismatch'}\n        </button>\n      </div>\n\n      {mounted && (\n        <AnalogPassport\n          comparison={mismatchMode ? TEST_ONLY_COMPARISON : null}\n          selectedAnalog={mismatchMode ? TEST_ONLY_COMPARISON.analogs[0] : null}\n          profile={mismatchMode ? TEST_ONLY_MISMATCH_PROFILE : null}\n          sources={mismatchMode ? TEST_ONLY_SOURCES : []}\n          loading={false}\n          errorMessage={null}\n        />\n      )}\n    </main>\n  );\n}\n\nconst rootEl = document.getElementById('harness-root');\nif (rootEl) {\n  createRoot(rootEl).render(\n    <StrictMode>\n      <HarnessApp />\n    </StrictMode>,\n  );\n}\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "tests/ui-sdk.spec.ts",
      "bytes": 22411,
      "sha256": "49ddeee75498c38e10731cc8ec31d569f998ef1420e5c34e4e01a8578aea526b",
      "content": "import { expect, test } from '@playwright/test';\nimport type { AddressInfo } from 'node:net';\nimport { createServer, type ViteDevServer } from 'vite';\nimport type { LocationsDataset } from '@shared/types/analog-location';\n\nconst EXPECTED_DISCLOSURE_LABELS = [\n  'Physical profile',\n  'Why this match',\n  'Limits of this comparison',\n  'Source evidence',\n] as const;\n\n// Strictly TEST_ONLY dataset fixture for verifying real AnalogPassport DOM behavior without public/data files.\nfunction makeTestOnlyFeature<U extends 'deg' | '1' | 'm' | 'degC' | 'kWh/m2/day'>(\n  unit: U,\n  quantity: string,\n  value: number | null,\n  sourceIds: string[],\n  missingReason: string | null,\n) {\n  if (value === null) {\n    return {\n      unit,\n      quantity,\n      method: 'TEST_ONLY_METHOD',\n      spatial_resolution_m: null,\n      support_window_m: null,\n      period: null,\n      value: null as null,\n      source_ids: sourceIds,\n      missing_reason: missingReason ?? 'TEST_ONLY missing reason',\n    };\n  }\n  return {\n    unit,\n    quantity,\n    method: 'TEST_ONLY_METHOD',\n    spatial_resolution_m: 30,\n    support_window_m: 500,\n    period: { start: '2024-01-01T00:00:00Z', end: '2024-12-31T00:00:00Z' },\n    value,\n    source_ids: [sourceIds[0] ?? 'src_test_https', ...sourceIds.slice(1)] as [string, ...string[]],\n    missing_reason: null as null,\n  };\n}\n\nconst TEST_ONLY_POPULATED_DATASET: LocationsDataset = {\n  schema_version: '1.0.0',\n  generated_at: '2026-01-01T00:00:00Z',\n  sources: [\n    {\n      id: 'src_test_https',\n      provider: 'TEST_ONLY Planetary Archive',\n      product_id: 'TEST_ONLY_LRO_DEM',\n      product_url: 'https://example.org/products/test-only-dem',\n      mission: 'TEST_ONLY LRO',\n      access_url: 'https://example.org/archive',\n      retrieved_at: '2026-01-01T00:00:00Z',\n      sha256: 'b'.repeat(64),\n      license: 'CC0-1.0',\n      local_path: 'raw/test_only_lro.tif',\n    },\n    {\n      id: 'src_test_unsafe_url',\n      provider: 'TEST_ONLY Credentialed URL Archive',\n      product_id: 'TEST_ONLY_UNSAFE_URL',\n      product_url: 'https://user:secret@example.org/unsafe',\n      mission: null,\n      access_url: 'https://example.org/archive',\n      retrieved_at: '2026-01-01T00:00:00Z',\n      sha256: 'c'.repeat(64),\n      license: 'CC-BY-4.0',\n      local_path: 'raw/test_only_earth.tif',\n    },\n  ],\n  profiles: [\n    {\n      id: 'lunar_south_pole_test_only',\n      version: '1.0.0',\n      algorithm: 'weighted_cosine_euclidean_v1',\n      alpha: 0.5,\n      features: [\n        {\n          key: 'slope_mean_deg',\n          unit: 'deg',\n          quantity: 'mean slope',\n          weight: 0.6,\n          lower: 0,\n          upper: 45,\n          calibration_source_ids: ['src_test_https'],\n          calibration_method: 'TEST_ONLY quantile',\n        },\n        {\n          key: 'solar_irradiance_kwh_m2',\n          unit: 'kWh/m2/day',\n          quantity: 'mean daily solar irradiance',\n          weight: 0.4,\n          lower: 0,\n          upper: 10,\n          calibration_source_ids: ['src_test_https'],\n          calibration_method: 'TEST_ONLY quantile',\n        },\n      ],\n      comparison_resolution_m: 30,\n      window_m: 500,\n      min_valid_area_fraction: 0.85,\n    },\n  ],\n  comparisons: [\n    {\n      profile_id: 'lunar_south_pole_test_only',\n      target: {\n        id: 'test_only_moon_site',\n        name: 'TEST_ONLY Shackleton Rim',\n        body: 'moon',\n        coordinates: {\n          latitude_deg: -89.5,\n          longitude_deg: 0.0,\n          frame: 'moon_iau_planetocentric',\n          source_ids: ['src_test_https'],\n        },\n        aoi: { local_path: '/assets/aoi/moon_test.geojson', source_ids: ['src_test_https'] },\n        missions: ['TEST_ONLY LRO'],\n        features: {\n          slope_mean_deg: makeTestOnlyFeature('deg', 'mean slope', 12.5, ['src_test_https'], null),\n          slope_max_deg: makeTestOnlyFeature('deg', 'max slope', 28.0, ['src_test_https'], null),\n          roughness_index: makeTestOnlyFeature('1', 'surface roughness', 0.42, ['src_test_https'], null),\n          elevation_delta_m: makeTestOnlyFeature('m', 'elevation relief', 420, ['src_test_https'], null),\n          temp_min_c: makeTestOnlyFeature('degC', 'minimum temperature', -210, ['src_test_https'], null),\n          temp_max_c: makeTestOnlyFeature('degC', 'maximum temperature', -55, ['src_test_https'], null),\n          solar_irradiance_kwh_m2: makeTestOnlyFeature(\n            'kWh/m2/day',\n            'mean daily solar irradiation',\n            1.4,\n            ['src_test_https'],\n            null,\n          ),\n          aridity_index: makeTestOnlyFeature(\n            '1',\n            'aridity index',\n            null,\n            [],\n            'Not applicable to airless lunar vacuum body',\n          ),\n          albedo: makeTestOnlyFeature('1', 'bolometric albedo', 0.14, ['src_test_https'], null),\n        },\n        geology: {\n          classes: ['anorthositic_regolith'],\n          method: 'TEST_ONLY spectral unit map',\n          source_ids: ['src_test_https'],\n          missing_reason: null,\n        },\n        assets: {\n          dem: null,\n          heightmap: null,\n          splat: {\n            asset: {\n              path: route.fulfill({ status: 404, body: 'Not Found' });\n    });\n\n    await page.goto('/');\n\n    const passport = page.getByTestId('analog-passport');\n    await expect(passport).toBeVisible();\n expect(trigger).toBeFocused();\n\n      await page.keyboard.press('Space');\n      await expect(trigger).toHaveAttribute('aria-expanded', 'true');\n      await expect(trigger).toBeFocused();\n\n 4).click();\n    }\n\n    await page.setViewportSize({ width: 1100, height: 760 });\n    await page.setViewportSize({ width: 1440, height: 900 });\n\n    if (webglErrorCount === 0) {\n      await 1;\n      if (requestNumber === 1) {\n        // Delay the first request so clicking Reload aborts it via AbortController cleanly.\n        await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 400));\n el.href : null;\n    });\n    expect(activeLinkHref).toBe('https://example.org/products/test-only-dem');\n    expect(pageErrors).toEqual([]);\n  });\n});\n\ntest.describe('UI SDK Agent Harness & React 19 StrictMode Mount Lifecycle (TEST_ONLY Harness)', () => {\n  let devServer: ViteDevServer | null = null;\n  let harnessBaseUrl = '';\n\n  test.beforeAll(async () => {\n    devServer = await createServer({\n      root: process.cwd(),\n      server: {\n        host: '127.0.0.1',\n        port: 0,\n        hmr: false,\n      },\n      logLevel: 'silent',\n    });\n    await devServer.listen();\n    const addr = devServer.httpServer?.address() as AddressInfo | null;\n    if (!addr) {\n      throw new Error('Failed to bind ephemeral Vite dev server for agent harness test');\n    }\n    harnessBaseUrl = `http://127.0.0.1:${addr.port}`;\n  });\n\n  test.afterAll(async () => {\n    if (devServer) {\n      await devServer.close();\n      devServer = null;\n    }\n  });\n\n  test('createAgent snapshot exposes 4 expected disclosures and executes available action to update DOM state', async ({\n    page,\n  }) => {\n    const pageErrors: string[] = [];\n    page.on('pageerror', (err) => pageErrors.push(err.message));\n\n    await page.goto(`${harnessBaseUrl}/tests/agent-harness.html`);\n    await expect(page.getByTestId('analog-passport')).toBeVisible();\n\n    const snapshotEntries = await page.evaluate(() => {\n      return window.__TEST_ONLY_AGENT__?.snapshot() ?? [];\n    });\n\n    const collapsibleEntries = snapshotEntries.filter((e) => e.component === 'collapsible');\n    expect(collapsibleEntries.map((e) => e.label)).toEqual([...EXPECTED_DISCLOSURE_LABELS]);\n\n    const targetEntry = collapsibleEntries.find((e) => e.label === 'Physical profile');\n    expect(targetEntry).toBeDefined();\n\n    // Execute toggle only if snapshot declares toggle; otherwise use the declared action (setOpen).\n    const actionResult = await page.evaluate(async (entry) => {\n      const agent = window.__TEST_ONLY_AGENT__;\n      if (!agent) return { ok: false, errorMessage: 'Missing agent' };\n      const chosenStep = entry.actions.includes('toggle')\n        ? { ref: entry.label ?? entry.ref, action: 'toggle', args: [] }\n        : { ref: entry.label ?? entry.ref, action: entry.actions[0] ?? 'setOpen', args: [true] };\n      return await agent.act({ steps: [chosenStep] });\n    }, targetEntry!);\n\n    expect(actionResult.ok).toBe(true);\n\n    const physicalTrigger = page\n      .getByTestId('passport-disclosures')\n      .locator('[data-slot=\"collapsible-trigger\"]')\n      .first();\n    await expect(physicalTrigger).toHaveAttribute('aria-expanded', 'true');\n    await expect(\n      page.getByTestId('passport-disclosures').locator('[data-slot=\"collapsible-panel\"]').first(),\n    ).toContainText('No prepared comparison yet');\n\n    // React 19 StrictMode unmount -> remount regression check.\n    await page.getByTestId('harness-toggle-mount').click();\n    await expect(page.getByTestId('analog-passport')).toHaveCount(0);\n    await page.getByTestId('harness-toggle-mount').click();\n    await expect(page.getByTestId('analog-passport')).toBeVisible();\n\n    // Scoring profile ID mismatch check: refuses mismatched analytics honestly.\n    await page.getByTestId('harness-toggle-mismatch').click();\n    const triggers = page\n      .getByTestId('passport-disclosures')\n      .locator('[data-slot=\"collapsible-trigger\"]');\n    await triggers.nth(1).click();\n    await expect(\n      page.getByTestId('passport-disclosures').locator('[data-slot=\"collapsible-panel\"]').first(),\n    ).toContainText('scoring profile mismatch');\n\n    expect(pageErrors).toEqual([]);\n  });\n});\n"

      "content": "import fs from 'node:fs';\nimport path from 'node:path';\nimport { expect, test } from '@playwright/test';\n\nconst DATASET_FILE = path.resolve(process.cwd(), 'public/data/locations_dataset.json');\n\nfunction hasGroundReadyDataset(): boolean {\n  if (!fs.existsSync(DATASET_FILE)) return false;\n  try {\n    const raw = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(DATASET_FILE, 'utf8'));\n    return (\n      Array.isArray(raw.comparisons) &&\n      raw.comparisons.some(\n        (c: { target?: { assets?: { heightmap?: unknown }; camera?: unknown } }) =>\n          Boolean(c?.target?.assets?.heightmap && c?.target?.camera),\n      )\n    );\n  } catch {\n    return false;\n  }\n}\n\ntest.describe('Planetary Analog Explorer - Empty / Error State & UX Disclosure', () => {\n  test('shows plain empty state with collapsed technical details, retry flow, keyboard focus, disabled reason, and zero external requests', async ({\n    page,\n  }) => {\n    const externalRequests: string[] = [];\n    let datasetRequests = 0;\n    page.on('request', (req) => {\n      const url = new URL(req.url());\n      if (url.hostname !== '127.0.0.1' && url.hostname !== 'localhost') {\n        externalRequests.push(req.url());\n      }\n      if (url.pathname.endsWith('/data/locations_dataset.json')) {\n        datasetRequests += 1;\n      }\n    });\n\n    await page.route('**/data/locations_dataset.json', (route) =>\n      route.fulfill({ status: 404, body: 'Not Found' }),\n    );\n\n    await page.goto('/');\n\n    const banner = page.getByTestId('dataset-error-banner');\n    await expect(banner).toBeVisible();\n    await expect(banner).toContainText('Prepared Data Unavailable');\n    await expect(banner).not.toContainText('3D-реконструкция');\n    await expect(page.getByTestId('target-selector')).toBeDisabled();\n\n    // Ground mode disabled + accessible visible reason\n    const groundBtn = page.getByTestId('mode-btn-ground');\n    await expect(groundBtn).toBeDisabled();\n    const disabledReason = page.getByTestId('ground-disabled-reason');\n    await expect(disabledReason).toBeVisible();\n    await expect(disabledReason).toContainText('Ground mode locked');\n\n    // Technical details disclosure starts collapsed, then expands to show diagnostic paths\n    const disclosure = page.getByTestId('technical-details-disclosure');\n    await expect(disclosure).toBeVisible();\n    await expect(disclosure).not.toHaveAttribute('open', '');\n    await disclosure.locator('summary').click();\n    await expect(disclosure).toHaveAttribute('open', '');\n    await expect(disclosure).toContainText('public/data/locations_dataset.json');\n\n    // Retry triggers a fresh dataset fetch\n    const beforeRetry = datasetRequests;\n    await page.getByTestId('retry-dataset-btn').click();\n    await expect.poll(() => datasetRequests).toBeGreaterThan(beforeRetry);\n\n    // Keyboard focus & activation path across controls and Pegman handle\n    await page.getByTestId('reload-dataset-btn').focus();\n    await expect(page.getByTestId('reload-dataset-btn')).toBeFocused();\n    await page.getByTestId('body-btn-mars').focus();\n    await expect(page.getByTestId('body-btn-mars')).toBeFocused();\n    await page.keyboard.press('Enter');\n    await expect(page.getByTestId('viewport3d-root')).toHaveAttribute('data-body', 'mars');\n\n    const pegman = page.getByTestId('pegman-handle');\n    await pegman.focus();\n    await expect(pegman).toBeFocused();\n    await page.keyboard.press('Enter');\n    await expect(page.getByTestId('viewport3d-orbit-notice')).toContainText(\n      'No prepared data for this area yet',\n    );\n\n    // Telemetry HUD displays the three distinct groups on desktop (1440x900)\n    await expect(page.getByTestId('hud-group-diagnostics')).toBeVisible();\n    await expect(page.getByTestId('hud-group-diagnostics')).toContainText('Not measured');\n    await expect(page.getByTestId('hud-group-preferences')).toBeVisible();\n    await expect(page.getByTestId('hud-group-scientific')).toBeVisible();\n\n    // Must never make requests to external origins\n    expect(externalRequests).toEqual([]);\n\n    // Verify WebGL initialized cleanly or surfaced explicit WebGL error\n    const webglErrorCount = await page.getByTestId('viewport3d-webgl-error').count();\n    if (webglErrorCount === 0) {\n      await expect(page.getByTestId('viewport3d-canvas')).toHaveCount(1);\n    }\n\n    // Resize checks must not duplicate canvas elements\n    await page.setViewportSize({ width: 960, height: 700 });\n    await page.setViewportSize({ width: 1440, height: 900 });\n    if (webglErrorCount === 0) {\n      await expect(page.getByTestId('viewport3d-canvas')).toHaveCount(1);\n    }\n  });\n\n  test('narrow viewport collapses telemetry by default, avoids horizontal overflow, and expands cleanly', async ({\n    page,\n  }) => {\n    await page.route('**/data/locations_dataset.json', (route) =>\n      route.fulfill({ status: 404, body: 'Not Found' }),\n    );\n\n    await page.setViewportSize({ width: 390, height: 844 });\n    await page.goto('/');\n\n    await expect(page.getByTestId('dataset-error-banner')).toBeVisible();\n    await expect(page.getByTestId('telemetry-hud-panel')).toHaveCount(0);\n\n    // Viewport canvas retains adequate space and no horizontal overflow occurs\n    const overflow = await page.evaluate(\n      () => document.documentElement.scrollWidth > window.innerWidth,\n    );\n    expect(overflow).toBe(false);\n\n    const rootBox = await page.getByTestId('viewport3d-root').boundingBox();\n    expect(rootBox).not.toBeNull();\n    expect(rootBox!.height).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(420);\n\n    // Expanding telemetry on mobile reveals the grouped HUD cleanly\n    await page.getByTestId('toggle-telemetry-btn').click();\n    await expect(page.getByTestId('telemetry-hud-panel')).toBeVisible();\n    await expect(page.getByTestId('hud-group-diagnostics')).toBeVisible();\n  });\n\n  test('rejects invalid JSON schema dataset with clear validation banner and collapsed details', async ({\n    page,\n  }) => {\n    await page.route('**/data/locations_dataset.json', (route) =>\n      route.fulfill({\n        status: 200,\n        contentType: 'application/json',\n        body: JSON.stringify({ schema_version: '0.0.0', invalid: true }),\n      }),\n    );\n\n    await page.goto('/');\n    await expect(page.getByTestId('dataset-error-banner')).toContainText('Dataset Validation Failed');\n    await expect(page.getByTestId('technical-details-disclosure')).not.toHaveAttribute('open', '');\n    await expect(page.getByTestId('mode-btn-ground')).toBeDisabled();\n  });\n});\n\ntest.describe('Planetary Analog Explorer - Real Dev_A Assets Integration', () => {\n  test.skip(\n    !hasGroundReadyDataset(),\n    'Real Dev_A dataset with target heightmap + camera not present in public/data/locations_dataset.json (ground integration NOT VERIFIED)',\n  );\n\n  test('supports target selection, orbit/ground switching, 20 rapid switches without canvas leaks, and splat failure DEM fallback', async ({\n    page,\n  }) => {\n    await page.goto('/');\n\n    const webglUnavailable = await page.getByTestId('viewport3d-webgl-error').count();\n    test.skip(webglUnavailable > 0, 'WebGL unavailable in test browser');\n\n    const selector = page.getByTestId('target-selector');\n    await expect(selector).toBeEnabled();\n\n    // Verify ground mode button becomes enabled when ground-ready target is selected\n    const groundBtn = page.getByTestId('mode-btn-ground');\n    const orbitBtn = page.getByTestId('mode-btn-orbit');\n    await expect(groundBtn).toBeEnabled();\n\n    // Perform 20 rapid mode switches and verify single owned canvas persists\n    for (let i = 0; i < 20; i += 1) {\n      if (i % 2 === 0) {\n        await });\n});\n"

      "content": "import tailwindcss from '@tailwindcss/vite';\nimport react from '@vitejs/plugin-react';\nimport crypto from 'node:crypto';\nimport fs from 'node:fs';\nimport path from 'node:path';\nimport type { IncomingMessage, ServerResponse } from 'node:http';\nimport { defineConfig, type Plugin } from 'vite';\n\nconst sharedDir = fs.existsSync(path.resolve(__dirname, '../shared'))\n  ? path.resolve(__dirname, '../shared')\n  : path.resolve(__dirname, './shared');\n\nfunction serveSourceBundle(req: IncomingMessage, res: ServerResponse, next: () => void): void {\n  const rawUrl = req.url ?? '';\n  const [urlPath, queryString = ''] = rawUrl.split('?');\n  if (req.method !== 'GET' && req.method !== 'HEAD') {\n    next();\n    return;\n  }\n\n  const bundlePath = path.resolve(__dirname, 'reports/source-bundle.json');\n\n  if (urlPath === '/__dev/source-bundle-meta.json') {\n    res.setHeader('Content-Type', 'application/json; charset=utf-8');\n    res.setHeader('Cache-Control', 'no-store');\n    if (!fs.existsSync(bundlePath)) {\n      res.statusCode = 404;\n      res.end(JSON.stringify({ available: false, error: 'reports/source-bundle.json not found' }));\n      return;\n    }\n    const body = fs.readFileSync(bundlePath);\n    const sha256 = crypto.createHash('sha256').update(body).digest('hex');\n    let fileCount = 0;\n    try {\n      const parsed = JSON.parse(body.toString('utf8')) as {\n        files?: unknown[];\n        file_count?: number;\n      };\n      fileCount = Array.isArray(parsed.files)\n        ? parsed.files.length\n        : Number(parsed.file_count ?? 0);\n    } catch {\n      fileCount = 0;\n    }\n    res.statusCode = 200;\n    res.end(\n      JSON.stringify({\n        available: true,\n        file_count: fileCount,\n        bytes: body.byteLength,\n        sha256,\n      }),\n    );\n    return;\n  }\n\n  if (urlPath !== '/__dev/source-bundle.json') {\n    next();\n    return;\n  }\n\n  if (!fs.existsSync(bundlePath)) {\n    res.statusCode = 404;\n    res.setHeader('Content-Type', 'application/json; charset=utf-8');\n    res.setHeader('Cache-Control', 'no-store');\n    res.end(JSON.stringify({ error: 'reports/source-bundle.json not found' }));\n    return;\n  }\n\n  const params = new URLSearchParams(queryString);\n  const viewInline = params.get('view') === '1';\n  const body = fs.readFileSync(bundlePath);\n  res.statusCode = 200;\n  res.setHeader('Content-Type', 'application/json; charset=utf-8');\n  res.setHeader(\n    'Content-Disposition',\n    viewInline\n      ? 'inline; filename=\"source-bundle.json\"'\n      : 'attachment; filename=\"source-bundle.json\"',\n  );\n  res.setHeader('Cache-Control', 'no-store');\n  res.setHeader('Content-Length', String(body.byteLength));\n  if (req.method === 'HEAD') {\n    res.end();\n    return;\n  }\n  res.end(body);\n}\n\nfunction devSourceBundlePlugin(): Plugin {\n  return {\n    name: 'pae-dev-source-bundle',\n    configureServer(server) {\n      server.middlewares.use(serveSourceBundle);\n    },\n  };\n}\n\nexport default defineConfig(() => {\n  return {\n    plugins: [react(), tailwindcss(), devSourceBundlePlugin()],\n    resolve: {\n      alias: {\n        '@': path.resolve(__dirname, '.'),\n        '@shared': sharedDir,\n      },\n    },\n    worker: {\n      format: 'es' as const,\n    },\n    server: {\n      // HMR is disabled in AI Studio via DISABLE_HMR env var.\n      // Do not modify—file watching is disabled to prevent flickering during agent edits.\n      hmr: process.env.DISABLE_HMR !== 'true',\n      // Disable file watching when DISABLE_HMR is true to save CPU during agent edits.\n      watch: process.env.DISABLE_HMR === 'true' ? null : {},\n    },\n  };\n});\n"
